
===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_submission_fairness.py =====
import inspect
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import ProductionStepAttempt, ModelConfig, ModelQuotaPool
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case


def test_shared_turns_reserve_normal_and_new_work_without_idle_capacity():
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import choose_ready
    rows = [dict(ticket=str(i), service=service, continuation=continuation,
                 ready_at=i, attempt_id=i)
            for i, (service, continuation) in enumerate(
                [('high', True)] * 6 + [('high', False)] * 2 + [('normal', True)] * 2, 1)]
    state, selected = None, []
    for _ in range(10):
        winner, state = choose_ready(rows, state)
        selected.append((winner['service'], winner['continuation']))
        rows.remove(winner)
    assert selected[:4] == [('high', True)] * 4
    assert selected[4] == ('normal', True)
    assert selected[5] == ('high', False)
    assert selected.count(('normal', True)) == 2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='state'):
        choose_ready([dict(ticket='x', service='high', continuation=True, ready_at=1, attempt_id=1)], {})


def test_ready_ticket_is_persisted_bound_to_lease_and_not_a_paid_invocation(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready, eligible_ready
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        model = db.scalar(select(ModelConfig))
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fair', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
        ticket = register_ready(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'],
            lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'],
            node=kwargs['node'], invocation_key='root:batch:2', now=now)
        db.commit()
        assert db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)) is None
        checkpoint = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).checkpoint_json
        assert kwargs['lease_token'] not in checkpoint
    with sessions() as db:
        rows = eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now)
        assert [row['ticket'] for row in rows] == [ticket]
        assert rows[0]['continuation'] is True
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now+timedelta(seconds=241)) == []
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        attempt.status = 'blocked'
        db.commit()
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now) == []
        attempt.status = 'running'
        attempt.lease_token = 'replacement-lease'
        db.commit()
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now) == []


def test_no_fairness_registration_when_disabled():
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready
    assert register_ready(None, attempt_id=1, lease_token='', worker_id='',
                          node={}, invocation_key='root') is None


def test_final_pool_gate_requires_prepared_ticket_and_persists_turn(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable
    from app.models import ModelQuotaLease
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='final-fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fair', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
        ticket = register_ready(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'], lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'],
            worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'], node=kwargs['node'], invocation_key='root', now=now)
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='no-ticket',
                                batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now)
        db.rollback()
        assert db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease.id)) is None
        db.rollback()
        reservation = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='with-ticket',
            batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now, fair_ticket=ticket)
        assert reservation.submission_state['fairness'] == {'turn':1,'normal':1,'high':0}
        db.rollback()


def test_shared_gate_rejects_losing_ticket_then_borrows_after_winner_cancelled(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready, check_ready
    from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='contended', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='contended', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        first = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        clone = ProductionStepAttempt(**{c.name: getattr(first, c.name)
            for c in ProductionStepAttempt.__table__.columns if c.name != 'id'})
        clone.step_key = 'fair-other'
        clone.idempotency_key = 'fair-other-key'
        db.add(clone)
        db.flush()
        other_id, pool_id = clone.id, pool.id
        db.commit()
        tokens = [register_ready(db, attempt_id=identity, lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'],
            worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'], node=kwargs['node'], invocation_key='root', now=now)
            for identity in (first.id, other_id)]
        db.commit()
    # A separate session sees the same ordering; no process-local arbiter.
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id)
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            check_ready(db, pool=pool, ticket=tokens[1], now=now, state=None)
        assert check_ready(db, pool=pool, ticket=tokens[0], now=now, state=None)['normal'] == 1
        db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).status = 'blocked'
        db.commit()
        assert check_ready(db, pool=pool, ticket=tokens[1], now=now, state=None)['normal'] == 1


def test_real_generator_registers_ticket_before_paid_boundary_and_settles(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    import asyncio
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import eligible_ready
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='consumer-fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='consumer', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
    class FakeClient:
        def __init__(self, config):
            self.config = config
        async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
            with sessions() as db:
                row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
                assert row.status == 'reserved'
                assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['batch_submission']['fairness']['turn'] == 1
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88,'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                raw_payload={'id':'fake'}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='fake',
                attempt_count=1, output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=80, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=100)
    async def scenario():
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1, phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1)) as runtime:
            return await runtime.run(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=FakeClient))
    result = asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert result.output_manifest['output']['score'] == 88
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        assert row.status == 'succeeded'
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)) == []


def test_paid_correction_rerun_is_exempt_from_the_shared_ticket_queue(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    """人工纠偏的付费重跑没有票据，也不该被公平队列挡住。

    2026-09-20：运营在 .137 对冻结在批量路线上的素材做字段纠偏，每次都收到
    「批量准备票据缺失或已过期，等待重新准备」。票据只有 Worker 在准备阶段登记，
    纠偏是在接口里同步重跑的，按定义就没有票据，于是 100% 失败。
    """
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable
    from app.models import ModelQuotaLease
    sessions, _kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='exempt-fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fair', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
    with sessions() as db:
        # 没票据又不豁免：照旧挡回去，自动链路的排队语义不变
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as blocked:
            acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='worker-no-ticket',
                                batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now)
        assert blocked.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
        db.rollback()
        assert db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease.id)) is None
        db.rollback()
        # 人工纠偏豁免：拿到名额，且不写入任何轮转状态（它本来就不在队里）
        reservation = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='paid-correction',
            batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now, fairness_exempt=True)
        assert 'fairness' not in reservation.submission_state
        db.rollback()
    # 豁免只跳过票据轮转，爬坡节流那一段照常
    assert 'fairness_exempt' in inspect.getsource(check_submission)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_submission_mysql.py =====
"""Real process competition in the explicitly disposable MySQL test schema."""
import json
import multiprocessing
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, release_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable
from app.models import ModelQuotaLease, WorkflowModelInvocation
NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 11, 4, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _submit(url, pool_id, run_id, attempt_id, now, start, ready_queue, result_queue):
    if url.host != '127.0.0.1' or not url.database.startswith('labelsystem_test_'):
        raise ValueError('only disposable loopback schemas allowed')
    engine = create_engine(url)
    event.listen(engine, 'connect', lambda c, _: configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(c))
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.connection()  # Establish every process before releasing the start barrier.
            ready_queue.put(attempt_id)
            assert start.wait(120), 'parent did not finish starting the isolated processes'
            try:
                quota = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key=f'call:{attempt_id}',
                    reserved_tokens=204096, now=now, batch_submission=True,
                    submission_estimate={'tokens':15000,'basis':'prepared-request-v1'})
                db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run_id, step_attempt_id=attempt_id,
                    node_instance_id='prompt', invocation_key='root', provider='doubao', model_id='test',
                    endpoint_id=f'ep-bi-category-{attempt_id%2}', quota_pool_id=pool_id,
                    quota_lease_token=quota.lease_token, reserved_input_tokens=204096,
                    reserved_output_tokens=0, estimated_cost_micros=1, created_at=now,
                    evidence_json=json.dumps({'reservation': {'batch_submission': quota.submission_state}})))
                release_model_quota(db, lease_token=quota.lease_token, now=now)
                db.commit()
                result_queue.put('admitted')
            except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                db.rollback()
                result_queue.put(exc.code)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_processes_share_batch_ramp_after_restart(mysql_engine):
    # Keep fixture imports in the parent. Spawn workers only need the real
    # quota/model code, not FastAPI and every test helper's transitive imports.
    from test_model_quota import _pool
    from test_runtime_long_tail_scheduling import ready
    from test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow
    import time
    with mysql_engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key='pacing-mysql', concurrency=24)
        pool_id = pool.id
        targets = []
        for i in range(8):
            attempt, _ = ready(db, version, f'pacing-{i}')
            targets.append((attempt.run_id, attempt.id))
        db.commit()
    ctx = multiprocessing.get_context('spawn')
    for wave in range(2):
        start, ready_queue, results = ctx.Event(), ctx.Queue(), ctx.Queue()
        children = [ctx.Process(target=_submit, args=(mysql_engine.url, pool_id,
            *targets[wave*4+i], NOW+timedelta(seconds=30*wave), start, ready_queue, results)) for i in range(4)]
        try:
            for child in children: child.start()
            deadline = time.monotonic()+120
            for _ in children:
                try:
                    ready_queue.get(timeout=max(1, deadline-time.monotonic()))
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise AssertionError(f'isolated worker startup failed: {[c.exitcode for c in children]}') from exc
            start.set()
            outcomes = [results.get(timeout=30) for _ in children]
            assert outcomes.count('admitted') == 1
            assert outcomes.count('model_batch_submission_wait') == 3
            for child in children:
                child.join(timeout=20)
                assert child.exitcode == 0
        finally:
            for child in children:
                if child.is_alive(): child.terminate()
                child.join(timeout=5)
            ready_queue.close()
            results.close()
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(ModelQuotaLease.status == 'active')) == 0
        for row in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)):
            state = json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['batch_submission']
            assert row.reserved_input_tokens == state['reserved_tokens'] == 204096
            assert state['pacing_tokens'] == 15000

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_submission_pacing.py =====
"""Shared submission pacing is distinct from in-flight concurrency (no API calls)."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable, release_model_quota
from app.models import ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaPool, WorkflowModelInvocation
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case, _drive_without_cooldown

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 11, 4, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.fixture
def database(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "pacing.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='batch-pacing', name='batch', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='test', max_concurrency=24,
            lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool)
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
    yield engine, pool_id
    engine.dispose()


def admit(db, pool_id, number, now, *, tokens=30000, endpoint='ep-bi-test', estimate=None):
    reservation = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id,
        owner_key=f'call:{number}', reserved_tokens=tokens, now=now,
        batch_submission=True, submission_estimate=estimate)
    # Same transaction as the production invocation writer: a second process
    # cannot acquire the pool before this submission evidence is committed.
    db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=number, step_attempt_id=number,
        invocation_key='root', node_instance_id='prompt', status='reserved',
        provider='doubao', model_id='test', endpoint_id=endpoint, quota_pool_id=pool_id,
        reserved_input_tokens=tokens, reserved_output_tokens=0, estimated_cost_micros=1,
        created_at=now, evidence_json=json.dumps({'reservation': {
            'batch_submission': reservation.submission_state}})))
    release_model_quota(db, lease_token=reservation.lease_token, now=now)
    db.commit()
    return reservation.submission_state


def test_idle_slots_do_not_allow_simultaneous_submissions_across_sessions(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            admit(db, pool, 2, NOW, endpoint='ep-bi-another-category')
        assert caught.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds >= 30
        # Even a caller committing after denial cannot leave a phantom lease.
        db.commit()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1
        assert admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30))


def test_heavy_request_uses_shared_slot_and_delays_following_request_online_bypasses(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=90000)
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=60), tokens=30000)
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds >= 60
        db.rollback()
        online = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool, owner_key='online', now=NOW)
        assert online.submission_state is None


def test_restart_does_not_reset_warmup_and_idle_does_not_accumulate_credit(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        later = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30))
        assert later['ramp_started_at'] == first['ramp_started_at']
        after_idle = admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(hours=6))
        assert after_idle['requests_per_minute'] <= first['requests_per_minute']
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            admit(db, pool, 4, NOW+timedelta(hours=6))


def test_live_warmup_grows_no_more_than_twenty_percent_per_three_minutes(database):
    engine, pool = database
    history = []
    now = NOW
    for number in range(1, 31):
        while True:
            with Session(engine) as db:
                try:
                    state = admit(db, pool, number, now)
                    break
                except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                    assert exc.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
                    now += timedelta(seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds)
        if history:
            previous = history[-1]
            for field in ('requests_per_minute', 'tokens_per_minute'):
                assert state[field] <= previous[field]*1.2+1e-6
            if state['tokens_per_minute'] > previous['tokens_per_minute']:
                assert state['ramp_started_at']-previous['ramp_started_at'] >= 180
        history.append(state)
    assert history[-1]['tokens_per_minute'] > history[0]['tokens_per_minute']


def test_new_provider_cooldown_reduces_rate_and_restart_keeps_reduction(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        state = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        row.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
        row.cooldown_reason = 'provider_rate_limited'
        db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        recovered = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=60))
        assert recovered['tokens_per_minute'] <= state['tokens_per_minute']/2
        assert recovered['requests_per_minute'] <= state['requests_per_minute']/2
    with Session(engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=61))
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds >= 59


def test_rereading_existing_reservation_does_not_pace_or_double_charge(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool, owner_key='existing',
            reserved_tokens=30000, now=NOW, batch_submission=True)
        db.commit()
        replay = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool, owner_key='existing',
            reserved_tokens=30000, now=NOW, batch_submission=True)
        assert replay.reused and replay.lease_token == first.lease_token
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('expired', [False, True])
def test_real_executor_parks_until_deadline_without_dispatch_or_attempt_charge(fallback_case, expired):
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.models import ContentIngressEvent, ModelConfig, ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionRun, RuntimeDispatchItem
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='pacer', name='pacer', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='pacer', max_concurrency=24,
            lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, 7001)
        event.received_at = now-timedelta(hours=24)+timedelta(seconds=-1 if expired else 5)
        admit(db, pool.id, 9999, now)
        original_hash = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).run_id).snapshot_hash
    calls = []
    class Client:
        def __init__(self, config): self.config = config
        async def chat_json(self, *args, **options):
            calls.append(self.config.endpoint_id)
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88, 'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{}', raw_payload={},
                upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='offline', attempt_count=1,
                output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=80, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=100)
    if expired:
        result = _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
        assert result.output_manifest['output']['score'] == 88
        assert len(calls) == 1 and not calls[0].startswith('ep-bi-')
    else:
        with pytest.raises(executor.FixtureExecutionError) as caught:
            _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
        assert caught.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
        assert 0 < caught.value.retry_after_seconds <= 5
        assert calls == []
        executor._report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'],
            lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], exc=caught.value)
        with sessions() as db:
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
            assert attempt.status == 'retryable' and attempt.attempt_no == 1 and attempt.lease_token is None
            dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id))
            assert dispatch.status == 'queued' and dispatch.lease_token is None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id).snapshot_hash == original_hash


def test_pacing_evidence_is_written_by_real_budget_reservation(fallback_case):
    from app.models import ModelConfig
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
    from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='real', name='real', provider='doubao', scope_kind='account',
            scope_identifier='real', max_concurrency=24, lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        first = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'], node=kwargs['node'],
            run_snapshot=kwargs['run_snapshot'], endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch')
        db.commit()

        assert evidence['version'] == 'batch-submission-pacing-v1'
        assert evidence['algorithm_version'] == 'prepared-token-pacing-v2'
        assert evidence['reserved_tokens'] == first.reserved_input_tokens+first.reserved_output_tokens


def test_required_long_spacing_is_not_mistaken_for_idle_and_can_recover(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(requests_per_minute=.25, tokens_per_minute=7500)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
        db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        next_state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=240))
        assert .25 < next_state['requests_per_minute'] <= .3
        assert 7500 < next_state['tokens_per_minute'] <= 9000


def test_financial_upper_bound_does_not_delay_small_prepared_request(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        state = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW, tokens=204096,
            estimate={'tokens': 15000, 'basis': 'prepared-request-v1'})
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        assert row.reserved_input_tokens == 204096
        assert state['reserved_tokens'] == 204096
        assert state['pacing_tokens'] == 15000
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=204096,
            estimate={'tokens': 15000, 'basis': 'prepared-request-v1'})


def test_cooldown_recovery_clock_is_not_reset_by_denied_checks(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW, tokens=600000)
        row = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        row.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
        row.cooldown_reason = 'provider_rate_limited'
        db.commit()
    for seconds in (60, 180, 240):
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
                admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds))
            db.rollback()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # After cooldown + 180 seconds recovery has begun even though no
        # reservation could yet persist the observed cooldown.
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1000))
        assert state['tokens_per_minute'] == 36000
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == 1.2


@pytest.mark.parametrize('settled', [False, True])
def test_v1_upgrade_keeps_rate_and_uses_only_known_usage_for_previous_weight(database, settled):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW, tokens=204096)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        ev = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        state = ev['reservation']['batch_submission']
        state['version'] = 'batch-submission-pacing-v1'
        state.pop('pacing_tokens', None)
        state.pop('estimate', None)
        state.pop('algorithm_version', None)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(ev)
        if settled:
            row.status, row.input_tokens, row.output_tokens = 'succeeded', 10000, 500
        db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        if settled:
            state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=204096)
            assert state['requests_per_minute'] == 2
        else:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
                admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=204096,
                    estimate={'tokens':15000, 'basis':'prepared-request-v1'})


def test_prepared_token_estimate_excludes_base64_and_includes_schema_and_each_image():
    from types import SimpleNamespace as N
    from app.batch_submission import estimate_prepared_submission
    body = {'messages':[{'role':'user','content':[
        {'type':'text','text':'测试'},
        {'type':'image_url','image_url':{'url':'data:image/jpeg;base64,'+'A'*100000}}]}],
        'response_format':{'schema':{'description':'字段说明'}},'max_tokens':4096}
    prepared = N(serialized_provider_request=N(body=json.dumps(body).encode()),
        output_budget=4096, media_dispatch_identities=('target:0',),
        media_bundle=N(items=(N(role='target',ordinal=0,image_tokens=1280),)))
    estimate = estimate_prepared_submission(prepared)
    assert 5376 < estimate['tokens'] < 6500
    assert estimate['text_utf8_bytes'] > len('测试字段说明'.encode())
    assert estimate['image_tokens'] == 1280
    prepared.media_bundle.items[0].image_tokens = 0
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared) is None
    prepared.serialized_provider_request = None
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared) is None


def test_explicit_multiframe_and_anchor_images_each_contribute_tokens():
    from types import SimpleNamespace as N
    from app.batch_submission import estimate_prepared_submission
    image = {'type':'image_url','image_url':{'url':'data:image/jpeg;base64,aaa'}}
    prepared = N(serialized_provider_request=N(body=json.dumps({'messages':[{'content':[image]*3}]}).encode()),
        output_budget=1000, media_dispatch_identities=('target:0','target:1','anchor:0'),
        media_bundle=N(items=(N(role='target',ordinal=0,image_tokens=1280),
            N(role='target',ordinal=1,image_tokens=1280),N(role='anchor',ordinal=0,image_tokens=595))))
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared)['image_tokens'] == 3155
    prepared.media_dispatch_identities = ('target:0',)
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared) is None

def test_budget_ledger_remains_frozen_with_independent_submission_estimate(fallback_case):
    from app.models import ModelConfig
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
    from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='split', name='split', provider='doubao', scope_kind='account',
            scope_identifier='split', max_concurrency=24, lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool); db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        before = json.dumps(kwargs['run_snapshot'], sort_keys=True)
        row = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'], node=kwargs['node'],
            run_snapshot=kwargs['run_snapshot'], endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch',
            submission_estimate={'tokens':1000,'basis':'prepared-request-v1'})
        ev = json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']
        assert ev['max_input_tokens'] == row.reserved_input_tokens
        assert ev['max_output_tokens'] == row.reserved_output_tokens
        assert ev['estimated_cost_micros'] == row.estimated_cost_micros
        assert ev['batch_submission']['pacing_tokens'] == 1000
        assert row.reserved_input_tokens+row.reserved_output_tokens > 1000
        assert json.dumps(kwargs['run_snapshot'], sort_keys=True) == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize('fallback_case', [True], indirect=True)
def test_real_explicit_image_executor_persists_traffic_estimate_and_original_budget(fallback_case):
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.models import ModelConfig, ProductionRun
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='prepared', name='prepared', provider='doubao', scope_kind='account',
            scope_identifier='prepared', max_concurrency=24, lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool); db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        original_hash = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).snapshot_hash
        db.commit()
    calls = []
    class Client:
        def __init__(self, config): self.config = config
        async def chat_json_images(self, *args, **options):
            calls.append(options)
            assert options['prepared_request'].sample_count == 1
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88,'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                raw_payload={'id':'fake-batch'}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='fake-batch',
                output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, attempt_count=1,
                input_tokens=1000, output_tokens=50, total_tokens=1050)
    result = _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
    assert result.output_manifest['output']['score'] == 88 and len(calls) == 1
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        assert row.status == 'succeeded'
        state = json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['batch_submission']
        assert 0 < state['pacing_tokens'] < row.reserved_input_tokens+row.reserved_output_tokens
        assert state['estimate']['image_count'] == 1 and state['estimate']['image_tokens'] > 0
        assert state['reserved_tokens'] == row.reserved_input_tokens+row.reserved_output_tokens
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).snapshot_hash == original_hash

import pytest

  attempt_no=attempt,error_type=kind,complete_parent=parent)==expected

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_worker_crash_settlement.py =====
"""A killed batch waiter must settle unknown after the worker liveness lease."""
import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem,
                        WorkflowModelInvocation, ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaPool,
                        Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem)


def _waiting_worker(root):
    # This is a genuine separate process. No provider or external network is possible.
    def deny(*args, **kwargs):
        raise AssertionError('external networking forbidden in crash fixture')
    socket.socket.connect = deny
    socket.socket.connect_ex = deny
    socket.create_connection = deny
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    engine = create_engine('sqlite:///' + str(root / 'crash.sqlite'))
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    executor.SessionLocal = sessions
    with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as patch:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(db, root, patch)
            pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key="crash-fixture", name="crash-fixture", provider="doubao",
                                  scope_kind="account", scope_identifier="fixture", max_concurrency=2, lease_seconds=86460, created_by="test")
            db.add(pool); db.flush()
            model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
            model.timeout_seconds = 86400
            node['retry_policy']['max_attempts'] = 3
            frozen_model = node['config']['model_snapshot']
            frozen_model['runtime_config']['timeout_seconds'] = 86400
            frozen_model.pop('snapshot_hash', None)
            frozen_model['snapshot_hash'] = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(frozen_model).encode()).hexdigest()
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'][0] = node
            asset = db.scalar(select(Asset))
            baseline = BaselineSet(name='crash-fixture', category_key=run.category_key,
                                   default_expected_level='L2', fingerprint='a'*64, created_by='test')
            db.add(baseline); db.flush()
            sample = BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id,
                                     expected_level='L2', asset_snapshot_json='{}')
            db.add(sample); db.flush()
            parent = BaselineRegressionRun(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, sequence_no=1,
                workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id, execution_engine='native_workflow',
                category_key=run.category_key, strategy_snapshot_json='{}',
                baseline_set_fingerprint=baseline.fingerprint, total=1, created_by='test')
            db.add(parent); db.flush()
            item = BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=parent.id, baseline_set_item_id=sample.id,
                asset_id=asset.id, expected_level='L2', production_run_id=run.id)
            db.add(item); db.flush()
            run.source_id = item.id
            snapshot['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id'] = parent.id
            snapshot['runtime_context']['baseline_item_id'] = item.id
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
            attempt_id, token = attempt.id, attempt.lease_token
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            db.commit()

        class WaitingClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                assert config.timeout_seconds == 86400

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
                # The real executor has committed its invocation and heartbeat by here.
                (root / 'waiting.json').write_text(json.dumps({
                    'at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    'attempt_id': attempt_id,
                    'token': token,
                }))
                await asyncio.Event().wait()

        executor._real_prompt_result(
            worker_id='worker-1', attempt_id=attempt_id, lease_token=token,
            run_snapshot=snapshot, node=node, input_manifest=input_manifest,
            model_client_type=WaitingClient,
        )


def test_killed_batch_waiter_recovers_at_301_seconds_without_resend(tmp_path):
    backend = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    child_env = dict(os.environ)
    child_env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join([str(backend), str(backend / "tests"), child_env.get("PYTHONPATH", "")])
    worker = subprocess.Popen(
        [sys.executable, __file__, '--waiting-worker', str(tmp_path)],
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, env=child_env,
    )
    try:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 20
        marker = tmp_path / 'waiting.json'
        while not marker.exists() and worker.poll() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            time.sleep(.05)
        if not marker.exists():
            worker.kill()
            stdout, stderr = worker.communicate(timeout=5)
            pytest.fail(f'worker never entered model wait: {stdout} {stderr}')
        waiting = json.loads(marker.read_text())
        engine = create_engine('sqlite:///' + str(tmp_path / 'crash.sqlite'))
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation.status == 'reserved'
            estimate = invocation.estimated_cost_micros
            assert estimate > 0
            invocation_id = invocation.id
            quota = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease))
            assert quota is not None and quota.status == 'active'
            assert (quota.expires_at - quota.acquired_at).total_seconds() == 86460
        worker.kill()
        worker.wait(timeout=5)
        assert worker.returncode != 0
        recovery_now = datetime.fromisoformat(waiting['at']) + timedelta(seconds=301)
        from app.workflow_runtime import recover_expired_runtime_steps, claim_next_runtime_step
        with Session(engine) as db:
            recovered = recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=recovery_now)
            db.commit()
            assert recovered == 1, 'dead worker still owns a batch-duration runtime lease'
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
            assert invocation.status == 'failed'
            assert invocation.error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
            assert invocation.estimated_cost_micros == estimate
            # Baseline runs retain the unknown estimate in their invocation ledger;
            # the incremental-only daily-budget table is not used by this source.
            assert invocation.reserved_input_tokens > 0 and invocation.reserved_output_tokens > 0
            assert invocation.actual_cost_micros == 0
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, waiting['attempt_id'])
            assert attempt.status == 'failed' and attempt.lease_token is None
            assert attempt.lease_expires_at is None
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
            assert run.status == 'failed' and run.error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
            assert run.lease_expires_at is None
            parent = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun))
            item = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionItem))
            assert parent.status == 'failed' and parent.completed == parent.failed == 1
            assert item.status == 'failed' and item.error_message == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
            assert json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json)['normalized_result'] is None
            assert all(d.status not in {'leased','queued'} for d in db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem)))
            quotas = list(db.scalars(select(ModelQuotaLease)))
            assert len(quotas) == 1 and quotas[0].status == 'released'
            assert recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=recovery_now + timedelta(days=2)) == 0
            assert claim_next_runtime_step(db, 'replacement-worker', now=recovery_now, global_limit=2) is None
            db.commit()
            assert len(list(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)))) == 1
            assert len(list(db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt)))) == 1
            assert json.loads(attempt.output_manifest_json) == {}
            assert run.status == 'failed'
        from app.workflow_runtime import complete_runtime_step, WorkflowRuntimeError
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as stale:
                complete_runtime_step(db, waiting['attempt_id'], waiting['token'], {'score':88,'label':'late'}, now=recovery_now)
            assert stale.value.code in {'step_lease_stale', 'run_terminal_state'}
            db.rollback()
        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, waiting['attempt_id'])
            assert attempt.status == 'failed' and json.loads(attempt.output_manifest_json) == {}
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id).status == 'failed'
            assert db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id).error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
        engine.dispose()
    finally:
        if worker.poll() is None:
            worker.kill()
        worker.communicate(timeout=5)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    assert sys.argv[1] == '--waiting-worker'
    _waiting_worker(Path(sys.argv[2]))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_billing_local_aggregate.py =====
"""Keep billing status and Beijing-day boundaries intact across SQL dialects."""
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.volcengine_billing import local_day_aggregate


def test_local_billing_excludes_rejections_and_unsettled_costs():
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # This read model only needs these columns; no model/provider invocation.
        db.execute(text('''CREATE TABLE workflow_model_invocations (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, endpoint_id TEXT, created_at DATETIME,
            status TEXT, error_code TEXT, input_tokens INTEGER, output_tokens INTEGER,
            cached_input_tokens INTEGER, actual_cost_micros INTEGER, upstream_status_code INTEGER)'''))
        rows = [
            (1, 'ep-test', '2026-09-20 16:00:00.000000', 'succeeded', None, 100, 20, 10, 7, 200),
            # 带实际用量结算的失败行（如 schema 不符）：token 与费用都要计入
            (2, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 15:59:59.999999', 'failed', None, 50, 10, 0, 27, 200),
            # 未结算：reserved 行的费用不计
            (3, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'reserved', 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown', 0, 0, 0, 999, None),
            (4, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'failed', 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown', 0, 0, 0, 0, None),
            (5, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'rejected', None, 0, 0, 0, 999, None),
            (6, 'ep-other', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'succeeded', None, 1, 1, 0, 500, 200),
            (7, 'ep-test', '2026-09-20 15:59:59.999999', 'succeeded', None, 1, 1, 0, 500, 200),
            (8, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 16:00:00.000000', 'succeeded', None, 1, 1, 0, 500, 200),
            # 供应商 429 拒绝：确认未计费，不算调用次数
            (9, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 02:00:00.000000', 'failed', 'model_provider_429', 0, 0, 0, 0, 429),
        ]
        db.connection().exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)', rows)
        assert local_day_aggregate(db, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21') == {
            'calls': 4, 'input_tokens': 150, 'output_tokens': 30,
            'cached_input_tokens': 10, 'cost_micros': 7 + 27, 'unresolved_calls': 2,
        }
        assert local_day_aggregate(db, 'ep-empty', '2026-09-21') == {
            'calls': 0, 'input_tokens': 0, 'output_tokens': 0,
            'cached_input_tokens': 0, 'cost_micros': 0, 'unresolved_calls': 0,
        }
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_bounded_runtime.py =====
import asyncio
import threading
import time

import pytest


def test_native_executor_has_async_entry_point():
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor
    assert callable(getattr(workflow_fixture_executor, 'process_runtime_step_async', None))


def test_waiting_requests_release_phase_threads_and_obey_bound():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait

    async def scenario():
        live = peak = 0
        phase_threads = set()
        async def provider():
            nonlocal live, peak
            live += 1
            peak = max(peak, live)
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(.03)
                return 7
            finally:
                live -= 1
        def operation():
            phase_threads.add(threading.get_ident())
            result = yield ProviderWait(provider, timeout_seconds=1)
            phase_threads.add(threading.get_ident())
            return result
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=4, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            results = await asyncio.gather(*(runtime.run(operation()) for _ in range(12)))
        assert results == [7] * 12
        assert peak == 4  # A phase thread must not be held for each HTTP wait.
        assert len(phase_threads) == 1
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_cancellation_and_timeout_are_delivered_to_settlement():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait

    async def scenario():
        settled = []
        dispatched = asyncio.Event()
        async def provider():
            dispatched.set()
            await asyncio.Event().wait()
        def operation(timeout):
            try:
                yield ProviderWait(provider, timeout_seconds=timeout)
            except Exception:
                settled.append('unknown-no-replay')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation(30)))
            await dispatched.wait()
            task.cancel()
            with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await task
            await runtime.run(operation(.01))
        assert settled == ['unknown-no-replay', 'unknown-no-replay']
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_cancellation_during_preparation_finishes_phase_before_cleanup():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait
    async def scenario():
        started, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        facts = []
        async def provider():
            facts.append('dispatched')
        def operation():
            try:
                started.set()
                release.wait(2)
                facts.append('prepared')
                yield ProviderWait(provider, timeout_seconds=1)
            except Exception:
                facts.append('settled')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation()))
            while not started.is_set():
                await asyncio.sleep(.001)
            task.cancel()
            release.set()
            with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await task
        assert facts == ['prepared', 'settled']
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_provider_heartbeat_failure_stops_wait_and_settles():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait
    async def scenario():
        facts = []
        async def provider():
            try:
                await asyncio.Event().wait()
            finally:
                facts.append('http-closed')
        def heartbeat():
            raise RuntimeError('lease lost')
        def operation():
            try:
                yield ProviderWait(provider, 5, heartbeat, .01)
            except RuntimeError:
                facts.append('unknown')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            await runtime.run(operation())
        assert facts == ['http-closed', 'unknown']
    asyncio.run(scenario())


@pytest.mark.parametrize('case', [
    'test_real_native_worker_calls_one_model_and_completes_local_nodes',
    'test_materialized_design_case_real_graph_calls_two_images_then_case_prompt',
])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('split', [False, True])
def test_async_native_business_settlement(tmp_path, monkeypatch, case, split):
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    import test_native_workflow_real_regression as contracts
    if not hasattr(contracts, case):
        pytest.fail('real business contract name must exist: ' + case)
    contracts._available_test_model_secret.__wrapped__(monkeypatch)
    def execute(worker_id, **kwargs):
        from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
        from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
        async def run():
            async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1,
                    phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1) if split else None) as runtime:
                return await executor.process_runtime_step_async(worker_id, runtime=runtime, **kwargs)
        return asyncio.run(run())
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, 'process_runtime_step_once', execute)
    getattr(contracts, case)(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

def test_async_consumer_stops_claiming_and_cancels_inflight():
    from app.async_runtime_worker import consume
    async def scenario():
        stopped = threading.Event()
        live = peak = cancelled = 0
        async def step():
            nonlocal live, peak, cancelled
            live += 1
            peak = max(peak, live)
            try:
                if live == 3:
                    stopped.set()
                await asyncio.Event().wait()
            finally:
                live -= 1
                cancelled += 1
        await consume(step, slots=3, should_continue=lambda: not stopped.is_set(),
                      shutdown_seconds=.01, poll_seconds=.001)
        assert peak == 3
        assert live == 0 and cancelled == 3
    asyncio.run(scenario())


@pytest.mark.parametrize('case', [
    'test_real_prompt_provider_timeout_is_unknown_and_never_retryable',
    'test_real_prompt_client_preflight_failure_is_safe_to_retry',
    'test_real_prompt_heartbeats_parent_while_provider_is_still_running',
    'test_post_provider_database_failure_never_resends_paid_request',
    'test_real_prompt_settlement_completes_runtime_attempt_in_same_operation',
    'test_real_foreach_fail_open_closes_provider_call_when_settlement_fails',
])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('split', [False, True])
def test_async_preserves_paid_failure_contracts(tmp_path, monkeypatch, case, split):
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    import test_workflow_fixture_executor as contracts
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    def wrapper(generator):
        def execute(**kwargs):
            async def run():
                async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1,
                        phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1) if split else None) as runtime:
                    return await runtime.run(generator(**kwargs))
            return asyncio.run(run())
        return execute
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_real_prompt_result', wrapper(executor._real_prompt_steps))
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_real_foreach_result', wrapper(executor._real_foreach_steps))
    extra = {'failure_phase': 'settlement'} if case == 'test_post_provider_database_failure_never_resends_paid_request' else {}
    getattr(contracts, case)(tmp_path=tmp_path, monkeypatch=monkeypatch, **extra)

def test_lease_heartbeat_is_not_starved_by_image_preparation():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait
    async def scenario():
        provider_started = asyncio.Event()
        facts = []
        async def provider():
            provider_started.set()
            await asyncio.sleep(.04)
        def heartbeat():
            facts.append('heartbeat')
        def waiting():
            yield ProviderWait(provider, 1, heartbeat, .005)
        def preparing():
            time.sleep(.08)
            facts.append('prepared')
            if False:
                yield
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            wait = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(waiting()))
            await provider_started.wait()
            await asyncio.gather(wait, runtime.run(preparing()))
        assert facts.index('heartbeat') < facts.index('prepared')
    asyncio.run(scenario())
