def _run_model_3d_su_worker(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    *,
    dimensions: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
) -> dict[str, object]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    source_path = tmp_path / "model-3d-su.jpg"
    source_path.write_bytes(b"jpeg")
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=source_path.name,
        stored_name=source_path.name,
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=source_path.stat().st_size,
        sha256="9" * 64,
        category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        provider="custom-compatible",
        model_id="vision-model-3d-su",
        encrypted_api_key="credential-reference",
        high_risk_review_enabled=False,
        active=True,
    )
    db.add_all([asset, model])
    db.commit()
    seed_model_3d_su(db, SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT))
    db.commit()
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
        )
    )
    prompt_a = db.get(PromptVersion, profile.prompt_a_id)
    prompt_b = db.get(PromptVersion, profile.prompt_b_id)
    v3_bundle = worker.v3_authoritative_category(db, MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY)
    snapshot = _category_execution_snapshot(
        profile,
        prompt_a_id=prompt_a.id,
        prompt_b_id=prompt_b.id,
        model_config=model,
        v3_authoritative_bundle=v3_bundle,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
        category_profile_snapshot_json=snapshot,
        prompt_a_id=prompt_a.id,
        prompt_b_id=prompt_b.id,
        status="processing",
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    calls: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    class FakeClient:
        def __init__(self, _config) -> None:
            pass

        async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **_kwargs):
            calls.append((system_prompt, user_prompt))
            if len(calls) == 1:
                parsed = _precheck_payload()
                parsed["classification"]["primary_category"] = "家装"
            else:
                parsed = {
                    "aesthetic_score": 100,
                    "aesthetic_evidence": ["五维评测所依据的整体画面证据"],
                    "overall_evidence": ["五维评测所依据的整体画面证据"],
                    "aesthetic_confidence": 0.9,
                    "dimensions": dimensions,
                    "overall_note": "按五维锚点完成评审。",
                }
            return DoubaoResponse(
                parsed=parsed,
                raw_text=json.dumps(parsed, ensure_ascii=False),
                raw_payload=parsed,
            )

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "settings", SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path))
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "DoubaoClient", FakeClient)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "prepare_model_image",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: (source_path, "image/jpeg"),
    )
    try:
        asyncio.run(worker.evaluate_job(job.id))
        db.expire_all()
        result = db.query(EvaluationResult).filter_by(job_id=job.id).one()
        return {
            "calls": calls,
            "prompt_b_system": prompt_b.system_prompt,
            "job_status": db.get(EvaluationJob, job.id).status,
            "score": result.score,
            "level": result.level,
            "needs_review": result.needs_review,
            "prompt_b_version": result.prompt_b_version,
            "scoring": json.loads(result.scoring_json),
        }
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("rule_profile", "expected_score", "expected_level"),
    [
        (("severe_defect", "minor_defect"), 0, "L4"),
        (("severe_defect",), 20, "L4"),
        (("obvious_defect",), 50, "L3"),
        (("minor_defect",), 80, "L1"),
        ((), 100, "L1"),
    ],
)
def test_model_3d_su_worker_uses_rule_hits_and_preserves_evidence(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    rule_profile,
    expected_score,
    expected_level,
) -> None:
    outcome = _run_model_3d_su_worker(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        dimensions={
            key: {
                "hit_rules": [
                    {
                        "rule_id": rule_id,
                        "confidence": "high",
                        "evidence": f"{key} 命中 {rule_id}",
                    }
                    for rule_id in rule_profile
                ]
            }
            for key in MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS
        },
    )

    calls = outcome["calls"]
    assert outcome["job_status"] == "completed"
    assert len(calls) == 2
    assert "aesthetic_score" in calls[1][0]
    # system 由运营手选的调用B版本自己决定，不再被合同生成的正文冒名顶替。
    assert calls[1][0].startswith("你是 TPENG 标签实验台的 3D & SU 模型调用 B")
    # 规则清单与输出合同仍由服务端强制注入 user 侧，运营丢不掉也改不了形状。
    assert "必须逐条核验以下维度规则" in calls[1][1]
    assert "扣分规则" in calls[1][1]
    assert "hit_rules" in calls[1][1]
    assert "调用A预检字段" in calls[1][1]
    assert "{{" not in calls[1][1]
    assert "grade" not in calls[1][1]
    assert outcome["score"] == expected_score
    assert outcome["level"] == expected_level
    assert outcome["needs_review"] is False
    assert outcome["prompt_b_version"] == MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_B_VERSION
    assert outcome["scoring"]["dimension_scoring_mode"] == "rule_deduction"
    assert outcome["scoring"]["dimension_deduction_output"]["dimensions"][
        MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS[0]
    ]["hit_rules"] == [
        {
            "rule_id": rule_id,
            "confidence": "high",
            "evidence": f"{MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS[0]} 命中 {rule_id}",
        }
        for rule_id in rule_profile
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "malformation",
    ["missing_dimension", "extra_dimension", "unknown_rule", "empty_evidence"],
)
def test_model_3d_su_worker_safe_fallbacks_on_invalid_rule_output(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    malformation,
) -> None:
    dimensions = {
        key: {"hit_rules": []}
        for key in MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS
    }
    if malformation == "missing_dimension":
        dimensions.pop(MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS[-1])
    elif malformation == "extra_dimension":
        dimensions["unexpected_dimension"] = {
            "hit_rules": [],
        }
    elif malformation == "unknown_rule":
        dimensions[MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS[0]]["hit_rules"] = [
            {
                "rule_id": "not_configured",
                "confidence": "high",
                "evidence": "合同之外的规则",
            }
        ]
    else:
        dimensions[MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS[0]]["hit_rules"] = [
            {"rule_id": "minor_defect", "confidence": "high", "evidence": ""}
        ]
    outcome = _run_model_3d_su_worker(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        dimensions=dimensions,
    )

    assert outcome["job_status"] == "completed"
    assert outcome["score"] is None
    assert outcome["level"] is None
    assert outcome["needs_review"] is True
    assert outcome["scoring"]["scoring_mode"] == "v3_authoritative_failed"
    assert "调用B" in "；".join(outcome["scoring"]["review_reasons"])


def test_material_prompt_context_is_explicit_and_can_be_disabled() -> None:
    enabled = worker._category_prompt_context(
        category_key="material_image",
        preprocess_config={"material_focus": True},
        document_context=None,
        pdf_summary=None,
    )
    assert "纹理尺度" in enabled
    assert "接缝" in enabled
    disabled = worker._category_prompt_context(
        category_key="material_image",
        preprocess_config={"material_focus": False},
        document_context=None,
        pdf_summary=None,
    )
    assert "纹理尺度" not in disabled
    assert "接缝" not in disabled
    assert "仅提示词评测输出合同" in disabled
    assert "不得返回 dimensions" in disabled


def test_freeform_prompt_context_does_not_inject_behavior_rules() -> None:
    pipeline = default_pipeline("material_image")
    pipeline["prompt_context"] = {"instruction": "必须输出管理员指定格式"}
    context = worker._category_prompt_context(
        category_key="material_image",
        preprocess_config={"material_focus": True},
        document_context=None,
        pdf_summary=None,
        pipeline_config=pipeline,
        include_dimension_rules=False,
        freeform=True,
    )
    assert context == ""

                "document_type": "方案",
                "summary": "摘要",
                "key_points": [],
                "visual_findings": [],
                "risks": [],
                "confidence": 1.5,
            }
        )


def test_pdf_worker_summarizes_before_single_prompt_evaluation(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    source_path = tmp_path / "proposal.pdf"
    source_path.write_bytes(b"%PDF-test")
    preview_path = tmp_path / "proposal.png"
    preview_path.write_bytes(b"png")
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="proposal.pdf",
        stored_name=source_path.name,
        mime_type="application/pdf",
        size_bytes=source_path.stat().st_size,
        sha256="e" * 64,
    )
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        category_key="pdf_text",
        stage="A",
        name="PDF 单提示词",
        version="pdf-single-v1",
        system_prompt="return complete evaluation json",
        user_prompt="evaluate {{image_metadata}}",
        rubric_version="pdf-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        provider="custom-compatible",
        model_id="vision-v1",
        encrypted_api_key="credential-reference",
        high_risk_review_enabled=False,
    )
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key="pdf_text",
        display_name="PDF 方案文本",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["application/pdf"]',
        preprocess_config_json=(
            '{"preprocess":"pdf","max_pages":4,'
            '"max_text_chars":24000,"multimodal_summary":true}'
        ),
        rubric_version="pdf-rubric-v1",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, prompt, model, profile])
    db.flush()
    v3_bundle = add_active_v3_contract(db, "pdf_text")
    snapshot = _category_execution_snapshot(
        profile,
        prompt_a_id=prompt.id,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        model_config=model,
        v3_authoritative_bundle=v3_bundle,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        category_key="pdf_text",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=snapshot,
        prompt_a_id=prompt.id,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        status="processing",
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    calls: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    class FakeClient:
        def __init__(self, _config) -> None:
            pass

        async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **_kwargs):
            calls.append((system_prompt, user_prompt))
            if len(calls) == 1:
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={
                        "document_type": "室内方案",
                        "summary": "这是客厅材质与照明方案。",
                        "key_points": ["客厅", "材质"],
                        "visual_findings": ["页图包含空间效果图"],
                        "risks": [],
                        "confidence": 0.9,
                    },
                    raw_text="{}",
                    raw_payload={},
                )
            elif len(calls) == 2:
                parsed = _precheck_payload()
                parsed.update(
                    {
                        "aesthetic_score": 68,
                        "aesthetic_evidence": ["方案正文与页图信息完整"],
                        "predicted_level": "L3",
                        "predicted_score": 68,
                        "confidence": 0.86,
                        "reason": "方案正文与页图信息完整，整体达到普通可用水平。",
                    }
                )
            else:
                parsed = _aesthetic_payload()
            return DoubaoResponse(
                parsed=parsed,
                raw_text="{}",
                raw_payload=parsed,
            )

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "settings", SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path))
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "DoubaoClient", FakeClient)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "prepare_pdf_model_input",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: PdfPreprocessResult(
            preview_path,
            "image/png",
            {
                "schema_version": "pdf-preprocess-v2",
                "text": "客厅照明与材质说明",
                "page_count": 1,
                "rendered_pages": 1,
                "text_chars": 10,
                "ocr_status": "not_needed",
            },
        ),
    )
    try:
        asyncio.run(worker.evaluate_job(job.id))
        assert len(calls) == 3
        assert "多模态前处理器" in calls[0][0]
        assert "这是客厅材质与照明方案" in calls[1][1]
        assert "客厅照明与材质说明" in calls[1][1]
        assert "hit_rules" in calls[2][1]
        db.expire_all()
        completed_job = db.get(EvaluationJob, job.id)
        result = db.query(EvaluationResult).filter_by(job_id=job.id).one()
        preprocess = json.loads(result.preprocess_json)
        aesthetic = json.loads(result.aesthetic_json) if result.aesthetic_json else None
        scoring = json.loads(result.scoring_json)
        raw_response_a = json.loads(result.raw_response_a)
        assert completed_job.status == "completed"
        assert result.level in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}
        assert preprocess["category_key"] == "pdf_text"
        assert preprocess["multimodal_summary"]["status"] == "completed"
        assert preprocess["multimodal_summary"]["model_id"] == "vision-v1"
        assert isinstance(aesthetic, dict)
        assert scoring["scoring_mode"] == "v3_authoritative"
        assert scoring["dimension_mode"] == "none"
        assert scoring["formal"] is True
        assert scoring["dimension_scoring_mode"] == "rule_deduction"
        assert scoring["v3_context"]["contract"]["category_key"] == "pdf_text"
        assert "dimensions" not in raw_response_a
        assert "不得返回 dimensions" in calls[1][1]
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("mode", "selected_keys"),
    [
        ("all", []),
        ("selected", ["composition_viewpoint", "color_material"]),
        ("none", []),
    ],
)
def test_worker_uses_v3_contract_despite_legacy_dimension_mode(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    mode,
    selected_keys,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    source_path = tmp_path / f"room-{mode}.jpg"
    source_path.write_bytes(b"jpeg")
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=source_path.name,
        stored_name=source_path.name,
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=source_path.stat().st_size,
        sha256=("a" if mode == "all" else "b" if mode == "selected" else "c") * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="范围与分类",
        version=f"A-dimension-{mode}",
        system_prompt="return precheck json",
        user_prompt="evaluate {{image_metadata}}",
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
        status="published",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="美感维度",
        version=f"B-dimension-{mode}",
        system_prompt="return aesthetic json",
        user_prompt="evaluate {{precheck_json}} {{rubric_version}}",
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
        status="published",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        provider="custom-compatible",
        model_id=f"vision-{mode}",
        encrypted_api_key="credential-reference",
        high_risk_review_enabled=False,
    )
    pipeline = default_pipeline("space_image")
    pipeline["prompt_mode"] = "single" if mode == "none" else "ab"
    pipeline["dimensions"] = {
        "enabled": mode != "none",
        "mode": mode,
        "selected_keys": selected_keys,
    }
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key="space_image",
        display_name="空间图",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
        pipeline_config_json=json.dumps(pipeline, ensure_ascii=False),
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, prompt_a, prompt_b, model, profile])
    db.flush()
    v3_bundle = add_active_v3_contract(db)
    snapshot = _category_execution_snapshot(
        profile,
        prompt_a_id=prompt_a.id,
        prompt_b_id=None if mode == "none" else prompt_b.id,
        model_config=model,
        v3_authoritative_bundle=v3_bundle,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        category_key="space_image",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=snapshot,
        prompt_a_id=prompt_a.id,
        prompt_b_id=None if mode == "none" else prompt_b.id,
        status="processing",
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    calls: list[str] = []

    class FakeClient:
        def __init__(self, _config) -> None:
            pass

        async def chat_json(self, _system_prompt, user_prompt, **_kwargs):
            calls.append(user_prompt)
            parsed = _precheck_payload() if len(calls) == 1 else _aesthetic_payload()
            if len(calls) == 1 and mode == "none":
                parsed.update(
                    {
                        "predicted_level": "L3",
                        "predicted_score": 68,
                        "confidence": 0.86,
                        "reason": "主体、画质与空间表达整体达到普通可用水平。",
                    }
                )
            return DoubaoResponse(
                parsed=parsed,
                raw_text="{}",
                raw_payload=parsed,
            )

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "settings", SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path))
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "DoubaoClient", FakeClient)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "prepare_model_image",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: (source_path, "image/jpeg"),
    )
    try:
        asyncio.run(worker.evaluate_job(job.id))
        db.expire_all()
        result = db.query(EvaluationResult).filter_by(job_id=job.id).one()
        scoring = json.loads(result.scoring_json)
        aesthetic = (
            json.loads(result.aesthetic_json)
            if result.aesthetic_json
            else None
        )
        assert db.get(EvaluationJob, job.id).status == "completed"
        assert len(calls) == 2
        assert "hit_rules" in calls[1]
        assert scoring["dimension_mode"] == mode
        assert scoring["dimension_selection"]["mode"] == mode
        assert scoring["scoring_mode"] == "v3_authoritative"
        assert scoring["formal"] is True
        assert scoring["dimension_scoring_mode"] == "rule_deduction"
        assert scoring["v3_context"]["contract"]["category_key"] == "space_image"
        assert isinstance(aesthetic, dict)
        assert result.level in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_claim_lock_ordering.py =====
"""领取任务时，队列聚合必须在锁 queue_scheduler_state 那一行之前算完。

那一行只有 id=1 一条，每次领取都 FOR UPDATE。原先两条跨表聚合放在锁之后，
6 worker × 16 槽 = 96 个领取者就全在这一行上排队等聚合。2026-09-16 实测
52.5 小时 1,900,033 次行锁等待、平均 46ms。聚合只读且不依赖 state，提前读不改变语义。
"""
import pytest

import app.runtime_queue as rq


class _Stop(Exception):
    """记录完调用顺序就打断，不需要把整个领取流程跑完。"""


def test_queue_counts_run_before_the_scheduler_row_is_locked(monkeypatch):
    order = []

    def fake_counts(db, *, now):
        order.append("counts")
        # 有一条待领取，才会走到锁；全零会在锁前直接返回（见下面那条用例）。
        pending = {q: 0 for q in rq.QUEUE_CLASSES}
        pending["production_batch"] = 1
        return pending, {q: 0 for q in rq.QUEUE_CLASSES}

    def fake_state(db, policy):
        order.append("lock")
        raise _Stop

    monkeypatch.setattr(rq, "_queue_counts", fake_counts)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rq, "_scheduler_state", fake_state)

    with pytest.raises(_Stop):
        rq.claim_runtime_item(object(), rq.ClaimRequest(worker_id="w", global_limit=96, lease_seconds=30))

    assert order == ["counts", "lock"], order


def test_the_source_keeps_that_order():
    """防止以后有人「顺手」把两行换回去：锚定源码里两次调用的先后。"""

    import pathlib
    source = pathlib.Path(rq.__file__).read_text()
    body = source[source.index("def claim_runtime_item("):]
    assert body.index("_queue_counts(db, now=current)") < body.index("_scheduler_state(db, policy)")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("pending_total,running_total", [
    (0, 0),      # 空闲：没有任何待领取项
    (5, 96),     # 全局在飞已满
    (5, 200),
])
def test_idle_or_saturated_claim_never_touches_the_scheduler_row(monkeypatch, pending_total, running_total):
    """2026-09-17 正式服近乎空闲时 57 分钟 96,923 次 upsert + FOR UPDATE 调度行，平均 23.7 ms。

    没有可领取项或全局已满时 choose_queue 必然返回 None，也不改 deficit，所以领取
    必须在锁之前就返回。
    """
    touched = []

    def fake_counts(db, *, now):
        pending = {q: 0 for q in rq.QUEUE_CLASSES}
        running = {q: 0 for q in rq.QUEUE_CLASSES}
        pending["production_batch"] = pending_total
        running["production_batch"] = running_total
        return pending, running

    def fake_state(db, policy):
        touched.append("lock")
        raise _Stop

    monkeypatch.setattr(rq, "_queue_counts", fake_counts)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rq, "_scheduler_state", fake_state)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rq, "inflight_pressure", lambda db, *, now: (_ for _ in ()).throw(AssertionError("pressure read before deciding idle")))

    assert rq.claim_runtime_item(object(), rq.ClaimRequest(worker_id="w", global_limit=96, lease_seconds=30)) is None
    assert touched == []

===== FILE backend/tests/test_classification_level_map.py =====
"""分类定级表规则模块：查表定级、更具体行优先、短路规则由表生成、收尾规则层直接定级。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.classification_level_map import (
    ClassificationLevelMapError,
    resolve_classification_level,
    short_circuit_rules_from_classification_map,
    validate_classification_level_map_content,
)
from app.workflow_result_finalizer import _classification_rule_decision


def _content() -> dict:
    return {
        "format_version": "classification-level-map-v1",
        "enabled": True,
        "field_paths": {"primary": "classification.doc_primary", "secondary": "classification.doc_secondary", "tertiary": "classification.doc_tertiary"},
        "rows": [
            {"primary": "交通工具", "secondary": "", "tertiary": "", "group": "功能性模型", "level": "L2"},
            {"primary": "厨卫用品", "secondary": "厨房用品", "tertiary": "橱柜", "group": "软装配景", "level": ""},
            {"primary": "厨卫用品", "secondary": "厨房用品", "tertiary": "厨具", "group": "功能性模型", "level": "L3"},
            {"primary": "家装", "secondary": "", "tertiary": "", "group": "空间/建筑类", "level": ""},
        ],
        "group_track_map": {"空间/建筑类": "space_building", "软装配景": "soft_furnishing", "功能性模型": "functional_model"},
    }


def _precheck(p: str, s: str = "", t: str = "") -> dict:
    return {"classification": {"doc_primary": p, "doc_secondary": s, "doc_tertiary": t}}


def test_resolve_prefers_most_specific_row_and_maps_track() -> None:
    content = _content()
    validate_classification_level_map_content(content)
    hit = resolve_classification_level(content, _precheck("厨卫用品", "厨房用品", "厨具"))
    assert hit["level"] == "L3" and hit["rule_key"] == "clm:厨卫用品/厨房用品/厨具"
    hit = resolve_classification_level(content, _precheck("厨卫用品", "厨房用品", "橱柜"))
    assert hit["level"] is None and hit["track_key"] == "soft_furnishing"
    hit = resolve_classification_level(content, _precheck("交通工具"))
    assert hit["level"] == "L2" and hit["track_key"] == "functional_model"
    assert resolve_classification_level(content, _precheck("不存在")) is None
    assert resolve_classification_level(content, {}) is None


def test_short_circuit_rules_derive_from_rows_with_specific_first() -> None:
    rules = short_circuit_rules_from_classification_map(_content())
    keys = {r["rule_key"]: r for r in rules}
    assert set(keys) == {"clm:交通工具", "clm:厨卫用品/厨房用品/厨具"}
    assert keys["clm:厨卫用品/厨房用品/厨具"]["priority"] > keys["clm:交通工具"]["priority"]
    assert keys["clm:交通工具"]["when"] == {"path": "output.classification.doc_primary", "op": "eq", "value": "交通工具"}
    assert keys["clm:交通工具"]["target_level"] == "L2" and keys["clm:交通工具"]["derived_from"] == "classification_level_map"


def test_validation_rejects_bad_rows() -> None:
    bad = _content(); bad["rows"].append({"primary": "交通工具", "secondary": "", "tertiary": "", "group": "x", "level": "L2"})
    with pytest.raises(ClassificationLevelMapError) as exc:
        validate_classification_level_map_content(bad)
    assert exc.value.code == "row_duplicate"
    bad = _content(); bad["rows"][0]["level"] = "L9"
    with pytest.raises(ClassificationLevelMapError):
        validate_classification_level_map_content(bad)
    bad = _content(); bad["rows"][0] = {"primary": "a", "secondary": "", "tertiary": "c", "group": "g", "level": ""}
    with pytest.raises(ClassificationLevelMapError):
        validate_classification_level_map_content(bad)


def test_finalizer_rule_decision_shape() -> None:
    manifest = {"resource_policy": {"level_scale": {"levels": [{"level": "L1"}, {"level": "L2"}, {"level": "L3"}, {"level": "L4"}]}}}
    decision = _classification_rule_decision(manifest, {"classification_level_map": _content()}, _precheck("交通工具"))
    assert decision is not None
    assert decision["level"] == "L2" and decision["score"] is None and decision["needs_review"] is False
    assert decision["decision_source"] == "rule_module:classification_level_map"
    assert decision["short_circuit_decision"]["rule_key"] == "clm:交通工具"
    assert _classification_rule_decision(manifest, {"classification_level_map": _content()}, _precheck("家装")) is None
    assert _classification_rule_decision(manifest, {}, _precheck("交通工具")) is None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_compose_passes_wait_cap.py =====
"""runtime.env 里的批量等待上限必须真的进容器。

2026-09-16 发现：LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_WAIT_CAP_SECONDS=900 写在 runtime.env 里一整天，
compose 却没有把它透传给任何服务，worker 实际一直按默认 420 秒在跑。
"""
import pathlib

import yaml

COMPOSE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "docker-compose.server.yml"


def test_every_runtime_service_receives_the_wait_cap():
    services = yaml.safe_load(COMPOSE.read_text())["services"]
    for name in ("app", "worker-group", "migration"):
        env = services[name]["environment"]
        assert env.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_WAIT_CAP_SECONDS") == "${LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_WAIT_CAP_SECONDS:-}", name

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_adapter_registry.py =====
"""Registering an interface must not require knowing what a 64-hex digest is.

Before this, every new category went through an engineer because
``schema_fingerprint`` is a SHA-256 whose value depends on which adapter the
delivery code dispatches on. The registry names the adapters the platform can
actually execute and supplies the digest wherever only the platform can know it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

import pytest

from app.connector_adapter_registry import (
    ADAPTERS, FACTORY_LIST_CONFIRM_FINGERPRINT, FACTORY_SUBMIT_FINGERPRINT,
    adapter, as_payload, derive_fingerprint,
)


def test_every_entry_is_complete_and_unique():
    keys = [item.key for item in ADAPTERS]
    assert len(keys) == len(set(keys))
    for item in ADAPTERS:
        assert item.direction in {'upstream', 'downstream'}
        assert item.fingerprint_source in {'constant', 'live_schema', 'convention'}
        assert item.label and item.summary and item.locator_hint


def test_the_submit_digest_matches_the_adapter_that_verifies_it():
    # Computed here rather than imported, to keep listing adapters from dragging
    # in the delivery chain; this is the assertion that keeps them equal.
    from app.factory_submit_delivery import FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT
    assert FACTORY_SUBMIT_FINGERPRINT == FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT
    assert derive_fingerprint('factory-submit-v1', '') == FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT


def test_the_factory_upstream_digest_matches_how_the_gray_was_registered():
    # The gray's upstream contract was minted with exactly this value.
    assert FACTORY_LIST_CONFIRM_FINGERPRINT == hashlib.sha256(
        b'factory-list-confirm-material-v1').hexdigest()
    assert derive_fingerprint('factory-test-list-confirm-v1', '') == FACTORY_LIST_CONFIRM_FINGERPRINT


def test_a_live_schema_adapter_is_not_guessed_at():
    # Reading the real table needs the deployment-side DSN and table name; a
    # registration form is not the place to open that connection.
    assert adapter('sql-envelope-v1').fingerprint_source == 'live_schema'
    assert derive_fingerprint('sql-envelope-v1', 'logical.anything') is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize('key', ['', '   ', 'nope', 'sql-envelope-v2', None])
def test_an_unknown_adapter_supplies_nothing(key):
    assert adapter(key if key is not None else '') is None
    assert derive_fingerprint(key if key is not None else '', 'logical.x') is None


def test_the_executable_flag_matches_what_the_code_dispatches_on():
    """A contract can carry any key; only these actually deliver."""
    from app import connector_delivery, incremental_readiness
    dispatch = connector_delivery.__file__ and open(connector_delivery.__file__).read()
    readiness = open(incremental_readiness.__file__).read()
    for item in ADAPTERS:
        if not item.executable or item.direction != 'downstream':
            continue
        assert item.key in dispatch, item.key
        assert item.key in readiness, item.key
    # The placeholders must be honest about doing nothing.
    assert adapter('manual-push-v1').executable is False
    assert adapter('local-sql-envelope-v1').executable is False


def test_the_payload_tells_the_page_what_to_ask_for():
    rows = {item['adapter_key']: item for item in as_payload()}
    assert rows['factory-submit-v1']['fingerprint_supplied_by_platform'] is True
    assert rows['sql-envelope-v1']['fingerprint_supplied_by_platform'] is False
    # The environment the deployment must configure is named, not implied.
    assert 'LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_' in rows['factory-submit-v1']['environment_hint']
    assert 'LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_' in rows['sql-envelope-v1']['environment_hint']
    assert set(rows['factory-submit-v1']) == {
        'adapter_key', 'direction', 'label', 'summary', 'fingerprint_source',
        'fingerprint_supplied_by_platform', 'locator_hint', 'environment_hint', 'executable'}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_contract_dotted_keys.py =====
"""The public registration service accepts logical namespaces, never paths/SQL."""
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.connector_contracts import ConnectorContractError, create_connector_contract_version
from app.models import ConnectorContractVersion


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    ConnectorContractVersion.__table__.create(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as session:
            yield session
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def register(db, key, **changes):
    options = dict(contract_key=key, direction='upstream', category_key='model_3d_su',
        logical_locator='logical.synthetic.input', adapter_key='sql-envelope-v1',
        schema_fingerprint='a' * 64, field_mapping={'content_id': 'content_id'},
        cursor_contract={}, idempotency_contract={'field': 'event_id'},
        batch_size=25, timeout_seconds=30, owner='synthetic', status='active', created_by='synthetic')
    options.update(changes)
    return create_connector_contract_version(db, **options)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('key', [
    'logical.model_3d_su.input', 'logical.model_3d_su.output',
    'logical.design_case.input', 'logical.space_image.input.resume',
    'knowledge-graph-input', 'legacy_key-1', 'a', 'a' * 120,
    'a.' + 'b' * 118,
])
def test_public_registration_preserves_namespace_identity_and_versioning(db, key):
    first = register(db, key)
    assert first.contract_key == key and first.version == 1
    assert register(db, key).id == first.id
    changed = register(db, key, schema_fingerprint='b' * 64)
    assert changed.version == 2 and changed.id != first.id
    assert changed.contract_hash != first.contract_hash
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ConnectorContractVersion)) == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize('key', [
    '', '.', '.logical', 'logical.', 'logical..input', 'logical._input',
    'logical.-input', '../input', 'logical/input', 'logical\\input',
    "logical.input';drop table x--", 'logical.input;select', 'logical.%2e.input',
    'logical.输入', 'Logical.input', 'logical input', 'logical\ninput',
    'logical\x00input', 'a' * 121, 'a.' + 'b' * 119,
])
def test_public_registration_rejects_invalid_keys_before_persisting(db, key):
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorContractError) as error:
        register(db, key)
    assert error.value.code == 'connector_contract_invalid'
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ConnectorContractVersion)) == 0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_delivery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_delivery import (
    READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS,
    ConnectorDeliveryError,
    DeliveryAttempt,
    DeliveryBatch,
    DeliveryItem,
    DeliveryKey,
    DeliveryWriteResult,
    FixtureDownstreamAdapter,
    SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter,
    DownstreamSafetyEvidence,
    deliver_outbox_batch,
    replay_dead_letter_delivery,
)
from app.connector_governance import connector_pause_state, set_connector_pause_state
from app.daily_review_quota import build_daily_review_batch
from app.daily_review_selector import ReviewCandidate
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ConnectorPauseState,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.runtime_outbox import RuntimeOutboxRequest, enqueue_runtime_outbox


def _mechanism(db: Session) -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key="connector.delivery.fixture",
        name="Connector delivery fixture",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json='["knowledge_graph"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="native-v1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json="[]",
        edge_manifest_json="[]",
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["incremental"],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash="a" * 64,
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version


def _target(db: Session, *, timeout_seconds: int = 30):
    return create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key="knowledge-graph-output",
        direction="downstream",
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        logical_locator="logical.knowledge_graph.output",
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint="f" * 64,
        field_mapping={"content_id": "content_id"},
        cursor_contract={},
        idempotency_contract={"field": "business_idempotency_key"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
        owner="platform",
        status="active",
        created_by="tester",
    )


def _enqueue(
    db: Session,
    version: WorkflowVersion,
    target,
    suffix: str,
) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
    return enqueue_runtime_outbox(
        db,
        RuntimeOutboxRequest(
            event_key=f"delivery:{suffix}",
            target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=target.id,
            business_idempotency_key=f"knowledge_graph:{suffix}",
            mechanism_version_id=version.id,
            payload={"content_id": suffix, "result": {"route": "domestic"}},
            max_attempts=3,
        ),
    )


def test_partial_batch_retries_only_unconfirmed_events() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            events = [
                _enqueue(db, version, target, suffix)
                for suffix in ("a", "b", "c")
            ]
            db.commit()
            failed_key = "knowledge_graph:b"
            adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
                fail_once_keys={failed_key},
            )
            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            first = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target,
                worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=adapter,
                now=now,
            )
            db.commit()
            failed = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, events[1].id)
            assert failed is not None
            failed.available_at = now
            db.commit()
            second = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target,
                worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=adapter,
                now=now,
            )
            db.commit()

            assert len(first.delivered_event_ids) == 2
            assert first.retry_event_ids == (events[1].id,)
            assert second.delivered_event_ids == (events[1].id,)
            assert adapter.attempted_batches == [
                (
                    "knowledge_graph:a",
                    "knowledge_graph:b",
                    "knowledge_graph:c",
                ),
                ("knowledge_graph:b",),
            ]
            assert len(adapter.rows) == 3
            assert {
                db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id).status for event in events
            } == {"delivered"}
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeAuditEvent.id))) == 4
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _event_audits(db: Session, event_id: int) -> list[RuntimeAuditEvent]:
    return list(
        db.scalars(
            select(RuntimeAuditEvent)
            .where(
                RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_type == "runtime_outbox_event",
                RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_key == str(event_id),
            )
            .order_by(RuntimeAuditEvent.id)
        ).all()
    )


def test_readback_mismatch_never_completes_and_attempt_evidence_is_append_only() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target, "mismatch")
            db.commit()
            key = event.business_idempotency_key
            adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
                corrupt_readback_keys={key},
            )

            # 读回一直对不上：按投递重试，max_attempts=3 用完才进死信，期间从不完成。
            base = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            outcomes = []
            for attempt in range(3):
                result = deliver_outbox_batch(
                    db,
                    target,
                    worker_id="delivery-worker",
                    adapter=adapter,
                    now=base + timedelta(hours=attempt),
                )
                db.commit()
                outcomes.append(
                    (result.retry_event_ids, result.blocked_event_ids, result.delivered_event_ids)
                )
            stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id)

            assert outcomes == [
                ((event.id,), (), ()),
                ((event.id,), (), ()),
                ((), (event.id,), ()),
            ]
            assert stored is not None
            assert stored.status == "dead_lettered"
            assert stored.delivery_receipt_json == "{}"
            assert stored.last_error_code == "downstream_readback_mismatch"
            assert [item.action for item in _event_audits(db, event.id)] == ["delivery_failed"] * 3

            replayed = replay_dead_letter_delivery(
                db,
                event.id,
                actor="delivery-admin",
                reason="已修复下游读回配置，批准重新交付",
                now=base + timedelta(hours=3),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert replayed.status == "queued"

            clean_adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
            )
            recovered = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target,
                worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=clean_adapter,
                now=base + timedelta(hours=4),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert recovered.delivered_event_ids == (event.id,)
            assert [item.action for item in _event_audits(db, event.id)] == [
                "delivery_failed",
                "delivery_failed",
                "delivery_failed",
                "dead_letter_replayed",
                "delivery_succeeded",
            ]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_readback_not_yet_visible_is_retried_then_confirmed() -> None:
    # 10-01 事件 71215：工厂同一秒写入，读回还没看到就进了死信，重放后立刻确认。
    # 现在先按投递重试，下一次读回一致即送达，不再变成死信挡告警与发布窗口。
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target, "late-readback")
            db.commit()
            base = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            first = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target,
                worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                    schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
                    corrupt_readback_keys={event.business_idempotency_key},
                ),
                now=base,
            )
            db.commit()
            queued = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id)
            assert first.retry_event_ids == (event.id,)
            assert first.blocked_event_ids == ()
            assert queued is not None
            assert queued.status == "queued"
            assert queued.dead_lettered_at is None
            assert queued.last_error_code == "downstream_readback_mismatch"

            second = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target,
                worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=FixtureDownstreamAdapter(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint),
                now=base + timedelta(hours=1),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert second.delivered_event_ids == (event.id,)
            assert db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id).status == "delivered"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_confirm_delays_set_the_next_attempt_time() -> None:
    # 只读确认类重试要等下游落定：适配器给的 retry_after 与读回未确认的 30 秒都落到 available_at。
    class _Unconfirmed(FixtureDownstreamAdapter):
        def deliver(self, batch):
            return DeliveryAttempt(tuple(
                DeliveryWriteResult(item.key, False, error_code="fixture_submit_unconfirmed",
                                    retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=30)
                for item in batch.items))

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            unconfirmed = _enqueue(db, version, target, "unconfirmed")
            db.commit()
            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=1)  # 晚于入队时刻，事件已到期
            result = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db, target, worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=_Unconfirmed(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint), now=now,
            )
            db.commit()
            stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, unconfirmed.id)
            assert result.retry_event_ids == (unconfirmed.id,)
            assert stored.last_error_code == "fixture_submit_unconfirmed"
            assert stored.available_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) == now + timedelta(seconds=30)

            lagging = _enqueue(db, version, target, "readback-lag")
            db.commit()
            later = now + timedelta(hours=1)
            deliver_outbox_batch(
                db, target, worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                    schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
                    corrupt_readback_keys={lagging.business_idempotency_key},
                ),
                now=later,
            )
            db.commit()
            stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, lagging.id)
            assert stored.status == "queued"
            assert stored.available_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) == later + timedelta(
                seconds=READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_readback_exception_is_recorded_and_retried() -> None:
    class _ReadbackTimeout(FixtureDownstreamAdapter):
        def read_back(self, keys):
            raise TimeoutError("readback timed out")

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target, "readback-timeout")
            db.commit()
            result = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target,
                worker_id="delivery-worker",
                adapter=_ReadbackTimeout(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint),
                now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )
            db.commit()
            details = json.loads(_event_audits(db, event.id)[-1].details_json)

            assert result.retry_event_ids == (event.id,)
            assert details["error_code"] == "downstream_readback_mismatch"
            assert details["retryable"] is True
            assert details["read_back_error"] == "TimeoutError"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("least_privileged", "fingerprint", "expected_code"),
    [
        (False, "f" * 64, "downstream_permission_overbroad"),
        (True, "e" * 64, "downstream_schema_drift"),
    ],
)
def test_unsafe_target_is_blocked_before_claim(
    least_privileged: bool,
    fingerprint: str,
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target, "safe")
            db.commit()
            adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
                least_privileged=least_privileged,
            )

            with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError) as exc_info:
                deliver_outbox_batch(
                    db,
                    target,
                    worker_id="delivery-worker",
                    adapter=adapter,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == expected_code
            db.rollback()
            stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id)
            assert stored is not None and stored.status == "queued"
            assert stored.attempt_count == 0
            assert adapter.attempted_batches == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_delivery_claims_only_the_contract_version_frozen_by_outbox() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target_v1 = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target_v1, "frozen-v1")
            target_v2 = _target(db, timeout_seconds=45)
            db.commit()
            adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                schema_fingerprint=target_v1.schema_fingerprint,
            )

            wrong_version = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target_v2,
                worker_id="delivery-worker-v2",
                adapter=adapter,
            )
            correct_version = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                target_v1,
                worker_id="delivery-worker-v1",
                adapter=adapter,
            )

            assert wrong_version.claimed_event_ids == ()
            assert correct_version.delivered_event_ids == (event.id,)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

                    result_stratum="risk",

                        ("low_confidence",) if index < 60 else ("redline",)

            )

            )

            )

            assert stored.status == "queued"
            assert stored.attempt_count == 0
            assert adapter.attempted_batches == []
    finally:
