    row = derived["metadata"]["deduction_breakdown"]["rows"][0]
    assert row["hits"][0]["description"] is None
    assert row["point_deduction"] == 10
    nodes = deepcopy(contract["nodes"])
    original = deepcopy(nodes)
    annotate_deductions(nodes, {"dimension_evidence": {"mode": "bonus_cap_v2"}}, {})
    assert nodes == original

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_display_label_fallback.py =====
"""纠偏契约的展示层 label 必须永远含中文，否则整页退化成只读。

validate_correction_contract 要求每个节点的 label 和 description 含中文。工作流
冻结的严格输出 schema 只给字段名和类型，没有 title，于是历史契约里全是裸英文
label——2026-09-14 林周金实测 run333（灵感图，72 节点）46 项报「必须是非空中文
描述」，纠偏页整页只读，一个字段都改不了。

映射表永远会落后于新字段，所以这里既锁精确中文名，也锁兜底：漏一个字段不能再
让运营完全无法纠偏。
"""

from app.correction_contract import (
    correction_contract_hash,
    correction_display_contract,
    validate_correction_contract,
)


def _contract(nodes):
    return {
        "contract_version": "v1",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "nodes": nodes,
    }


def _node(node_key, label, **extra):
    node = {
        "node_key": node_key,
        "layer": "A",
        "path": node_key,
        "label": label,
        "description": "",
        "type": "text",
        "semantic_version": "1",
        "compatibility_key": node_key,
        "required": False,
        "evidence": {"description": "请填写人工证据", "required": False},
    }
    node.update(extra)
    return node


def test_inspiration_bare_english_labels_no_longer_force_read_only():
    """run333 的真实症状：整份契约因裸英文 label 判失败 → 纠偏页只读。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.classification_confidence", "classification_confidence"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.decision_status", "decision_status"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.uncertain_fields", "uncertain_fields"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_b.dimensions.color_aesthetics.grade", "grade"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_b.source_deductions", "source_deductions"),
    ])
    assert len(validate_correction_contract(contract)) > 0
    assert validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True) == []

    shown = {n["node_key"]: n["label"] for n in correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"]}
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.classification_confidence"] == "分类置信度"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.decision_status"] == "判定状态"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_b.dimensions.color_aesthetics.grade"] == "色彩美学 · 评级"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_b.source_deductions"] == "来源扣分"


def test_redline_children_reuse_existing_business_wording():
    """红线子项直接复用既有业务口径，不另起一套说法。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.screenshot", "screenshot"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.large_area_watermark", "large_area_watermark"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.upside_down_or_mirrored", "upside_down_or_mirrored"),
    ])
    shown = {n["node_key"]: n["label"] for n in correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"]}
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.screenshot"] == "红线命中：是截图"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.large_area_watermark"] == "红线命中：有大面积水印"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.upside_down_or_mirrored"] == "红线命中：是颠倒图"
    assert validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True) == []


def test_unknown_field_falls_back_instead_of_blanking_the_page():
    """映射表永远落后于新字段——漏一个也不能让整页只读。

    这是下限保证：兜底保留原字段名，运营仍能对上是哪一项。
    """

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.some_brand_new_field", "some_brand_new_field"),
    ])
    assert len(validate_correction_contract(contract)) > 0
    assert validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True) == []
    node = correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"][0]
    assert node["label"] == "提示词输出字段 some_brand_new_field"
    assert "some_brand_new_field" in node["label"]


def test_existing_chinese_labels_are_left_alone():
    """已中文化的 label 和作者写的 title 不被兜底覆盖。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.title", "运营自定义标题口径"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.decision_status", "本版专用判定状态"),
    ])
    shown = {n["node_key"]: n["label"] for n in correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"]}
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.title"] == "运营自定义标题口径"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.decision_status"] == "本版专用判定状态"


def test_display_overlay_never_touches_identity_or_field_set():
    """展示层只改 label/description：字段集合、类型和存库 hash 都不能变。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.decision_status", "decision_status"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.watermark", "watermark", type="enum"),
    ])
    before_hash = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    shown = correction_display_contract(contract)
    assert correction_contract_hash(contract) == before_hash  # 原对象未被就地改动
    assert [n["node_key"] for n in shown["nodes"]] == [n["node_key"] for n in contract["nodes"]]
    assert [n["type"] for n in shown["nodes"]] == [n["type"] for n in contract["nodes"]]


def test_model_3d_su_labels_still_win_over_the_fallback():
    """3D/SU 的精确中文名不能被兜底顶掉。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.watermark", "watermark", type="enum"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.qr_code", "qr_code", type="enum"),
    ])
    shown = {n["node_key"]: n["label"] for n in correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"]}
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.watermark"] == "水印"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.qr_code"] == "二维码"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_is_single_reroute.py =====
"""纠偏页填另一路线的 is_single：必须能改，并被翻译成一次路线改判（不是报错、不是只读）。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.correction_contract import (
    ContractValidationError,
    allowed_is_single_values,
    route_site_from_node_key,
    validate_node_value,
)


def _node(node_key: str, options: list[int]) -> dict:
    return {"node_key": node_key, "type": "enum", "options": options, "label": "是否单体"}


def test_is_single_accepts_the_other_scope_of_the_same_site() -> None:
    """海外整体节点冻结 options=[1]，运营填 2（单体）必须放行。"""

    overseas_whole = _node("workflow.prompt_overseas_whole.is_single", [1])
    validate_node_value(overseas_whole, 1)
    validate_node_value(overseas_whole, 2)  # 改判为海外单体
    with pytest.raises(ContractValidationError) as captured:
        validate_node_value(overseas_whole, 0)  # 0 是国内编码，海外链路无此值
    assert "海外链路只能填 1（整体）、2（单体）" in str(captured.value)
    assert "改判到对应路线" in str(captured.value)

    domestic_whole = _node("workflow.prompt_domestic_whole.is_single", [0])
    validate_node_value(domestic_whole, 1)
    with pytest.raises(ContractValidationError):
        validate_node_value(domestic_whole, 2)


def test_site_and_allowed_values_are_read_from_node_key() -> None:
    assert route_site_from_node_key("workflow.prompt_overseas_single.is_single") == "overseas"
    assert route_site_from_node_key("workflow.prompt_domestic_whole.is_single") == "domestic"
    assert route_site_from_node_key("workflow.prompt_a.score") is None
    assert allowed_is_single_values("workflow.prompt_overseas_whole.is_single") == [1, 2]
    assert allowed_is_single_values("workflow.prompt_domestic_whole.is_single") == [0, 1]
    assert allowed_is_single_values("workflow.prompt_a.score") is None


def test_other_enum_fields_keep_strict_validation() -> None:
    """放宽只针对 is_single，其他 enum 字段不受影响。"""

    node = {"node_key": "workflow.prompt_overseas_whole.color", "type": "enum", "options": ["white"], "label": "主色系"}
    validate_node_value(node, "white")
    with pytest.raises(ContractValidationError) as captured:
        validate_node_value(node, "black")
    assert "只能填 white" in str(captured.value)


def test_error_messages_are_human_readable() -> None:
    """报错要说清能填什么、当前填了什么，不再是「不符合合同约束」。"""

    node = {"node_key": "workflow.prompt_a.score", "type": "integer", "label": "模型评分"}
    with pytest.raises(ContractValidationError) as captured:
        validate_node_value(node, "高")
    message = str(captured.value)
    assert "「模型评分」必须是整数" in message and "高" in message
    assert "不符合合同约束" not in message


def _effective(contract_key: str, *, is_single: int, identity_field: str, identity: str | None):
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    return SimpleNamespace(
        truth_kind="structured_fields",
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        expected_result={identity_field: identity or "", "is_single": is_single, "space": "living room"},
        expected_level=None,
        workflow_input=(
            {"source": contract_key.split(".")[2], "is_single": is_single, identity_field: identity}
            if identity
            else {"source": contract_key.split(".")[2], "is_single": is_single}
        ),
    )


def _capture_route_calls(monkeypatch):
    import app.workflow_correction as wc

    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(wc, "revise_knowledge_graph_route", lambda db, **kw: calls.append(kw) or "REVISION")
    return wc, calls


def test_filling_the_other_scope_triggers_a_route_change(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    wc, calls = _capture_route_calls(monkeypatch)
    effective = _effective(
        "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1", is_single=1, identity_field="res_id", identity="6938137"
    )
    result = wc._route_revision_from_is_single(
        None,
        source_item=SimpleNamespace(id=5872),
        effective=effective,
        node_instance_id="prompt_overseas_whole",
        field_path="is_single",
        value=2,
        regression_item=SimpleNamespace(run_id=214),
        run=SimpleNamespace(id=6431),
        event_id="evt-1",
        reason="图上是两个花瓶摆件，应判为单体",
        actor="liangjx",
    )
    assert result == "REVISION"
    assert len(calls) == 1
    call = calls[0]
    assert call["baseline_set_item_id"] == 5872
    assert call["source"] == "overseas" and call["is_single"] == 2
    assert call["identity_value"] == "6938137"
    assert call["confirmed"] is True
    assert call["reason"] == "图上是两个花瓶摆件，应判为单体"
    assert call["source_baseline_run_id"] == 214 and call["source_production_run_id"] == 6431


def test_same_value_and_other_fields_fall_through_to_normal_confirmation(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    wc, calls = _capture_route_calls(monkeypatch)
    effective = _effective(
        "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1", is_single=1, identity_field="res_id", identity="6938137"
    )
    common = dict(
        source_item=SimpleNamespace(id=1),
        effective=effective,
        node_instance_id="prompt_overseas_whole",
        regression_item=SimpleNamespace(run_id=1),
        run=SimpleNamespace(id=1),
        event_id="e",
        reason="r",
        actor="a",
    )
    assert wc._route_revision_from_is_single(None, field_path="is_single", value=1, **common) is None
    assert wc._route_revision_from_is_single(None, field_path="color", value="white", **common) is None
    assert calls == []


def test_route_change_rejects_cross_site_and_missing_identity(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    wc, _ = _capture_route_calls(monkeypatch)
    overseas = _effective(
        "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1", is_single=1, identity_field="res_id", identity="6938137"
    )
    common = dict(
        node_instance_id="prompt_overseas_whole",
        field_path="is_single",
        regression_item=SimpleNamespace(run_id=1),
        run=SimpleNamespace(id=1),
        event_id="e",
        reason="r",
        actor="a",
    )
    with pytest.raises(wc.WorkflowCorrectionError) as cross:
        wc._route_revision_from_is_single(
            None, source_item=SimpleNamespace(id=1), effective=overseas, value=0, **common
        )
    assert "海外链路的整体/单体只能填 [1, 2]" in str(cross.value)

    without_identity = _effective(
        "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1", is_single=1, identity_field="res_id", identity=None
    )
    without_identity.expected_result["res_id"] = ""
    with pytest.raises(wc.WorkflowCorrectionError) as missing:
        wc._route_revision_from_is_single(
            None, source_item=SimpleNamespace(id=1), effective=without_identity, value=2, **common
        )
    assert "需要先有 res_id" in str(missing.value)


def test_output_prefixed_field_path_is_recognized(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """提交方可能带 output. 前缀，改判判定要一致。"""

    from types import SimpleNamespace

    wc, calls = _capture_route_calls(monkeypatch)
    effective = _effective(

        effective=effective,

        field_path="output.is_single",
        value=2,
        regression_item=SimpleNamespace(run_id=1),
        run=SimpleNamespace(id=1),
        event_id="e",
        reason="r",
        actor="a",
    )
    assert result == "REVISION" and len(calls) == 1


def test_route_correction_short_circuits_before_output_rewrite(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """改判必须在「改写本轮输出」之前分流：本轮结果不改写，只落真值。"""

    import json
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    import app.workflow_correction as wc

    normalized = {"res_id": "6938137", "is_single": 1}
    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(
        normalized_result_json=json.dumps(normalized), correction_history_json="[]"
    )
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=6431,
        source_type="baseline_regression_item",
        source_id=99,
        workflow_definition_id=2,
        workflow_version_id=210,
    )
    revision = SimpleNamespace(
        revision=3,
        canonical_hash="h" * 64,
        truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1",
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(wc, "_route_revision_from_is_single", lambda db, **kw: revision)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        wc,
        "resolve_effective_baseline_truth",
        lambda db, item: SimpleNamespace(
            truth_kind="structured_fields",
            truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1",
            expected_result={"res_id": "6938137", "is_single": 1},
        ),
    )

    class _DB:
        def get(self, model, pk):
            return SimpleNamespace(
                production_run_id=6431, run_id=214, baseline_set_item_id=5872,
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1",
                baseline_set_item=SimpleNamespace(id=5872),
            )

        def scalar(self, statement):
            assert "FOR UPDATE" in str(statement)
            return SimpleNamespace(id=5872)

        def flush(self):
            return None

    result = wc._route_correction_result(
        _DB(),
        run=run,
        evaluation=evaluation,
        node={"kind": "prompt", "config": {"component_snapshot": {"content_hash": "c" * 64}}},
        node_instance_id="prompt_overseas_whole",
        field_path="is_single",
        value=2,
        event_id="evt",
        reason="两个花瓶摆件，是单体",
        actor="liangjx",
    )
    assert result is not None
    assert result.affected_nodes == ()  # 本轮输出一个都没改
    assert result.normalized_result == normalized
    assert result.truth_revision == 3 and result.effective_next_run is True
    # 必须留下可审计的纠偏历史，否则上层按 correction_key 找不到会 409
    history = json.loads(evaluation.correction_history_json)
    assert len(history) == 1
    entry = history[0]
    assert entry["correction_key"] == "evt" and entry["new_value"] == 2
    assert entry["old_value"] == 1 and entry["affected_nodes"] == []
    assert entry["route_reassignment"] == {
        "field": "is_single",
        "from_contract_key": "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1",
        "to_contract_key": "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1",
        "is_single": 2,
        "truth_revision": 3,
    }
    assert entry["before_result_hash"] == entry["after_result_hash"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("site,old,expected", [("overseas",2,[1,2]),("domestic",0,[0,1])])
def test_correction_view_exposes_both_route_options_without_changing_contract(site,old,expected):
    from copy import deepcopy
    from app.correction_view import build_correction_nodes
    key=f"workflow.prompt_{site}_single.is_single"
    node={**_node(key,[old]), "semantic_version":"1", "compatibility_key":key,
          "metadata":{"editable":True}, "layer":"A", "path":key}
    contract={"nodes":[node]};before=deepcopy(contract)
    views=build_correction_nodes(contract,model_values={key:old},human_values={},previous_values={})
    assert views[0]["options"]==expected
    assert views[0]["model_value"]==old
    assert contract==before
    for value in expected:validate_node_value(node,value)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_notes.py =====
import pytest

from app.baseline_regression import _correction_context
from app.correction_notes import append_correction_note, correction_notes
from app.correction_view import CorrectionViewError, build_correction_view
from test_correction_view import _submission_fixture


def test_note_is_persistent_idempotent_and_does_not_create_a_value_correction():
    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    try:
        before = (item.evaluation.correction_history_json, item.evaluation.review_revision,
                  item.evaluation.precheck_json, item.evaluation.raw_response_a)
        count_before = _correction_context(item)['human_evidence_count']
        args = dict(run=run, item=item, text='细节边缘模糊，建议降低细节分',
                    idempotency_key='note-test-unique', actor='运营甲')
        first = append_correction_note(db, **args)
        db.commit()
        replay = append_correction_note(db, **args)
        assert not first['idempotent_replay'] and replay['idempotent_replay']
        assert len(replay['notes']) == 1
        assert before == (item.evaluation.correction_history_json, item.evaluation.review_revision,
                          item.evaluation.precheck_json, item.evaluation.raw_response_a)
        assert build_correction_view(db, run=run, item=item)['notes'][0]['text'] == args['text']
        context = _correction_context(item)
        assert context['human_evidence_count'] == count_before
        assert context['manual_notes'][0]['text'] == args['text']
        assert correction_notes(db, run_id=999, item_id=item.id) == []
        with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError, match='其他备注'):
            append_correction_note(db, **{**args, 'text': '不能覆盖旧备注'})
        with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError, match='其他备注'):
            append_correction_note(db, **{**args, 'actor': '另一位运营'})
    finally:
        db.close()


def test_note_rejects_blank_and_unrelated_items():
    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    try:
        args = dict(run=run, item=item, text=' ', idempotency_key='note-test-unique', actor='运营甲')
        with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError, match='2000'):
            append_correction_note(db, **args)
        item.run_id = 999
        with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError, match='评测结果'):
            append_correction_note(db, **{**args, 'text': '备注'})
    finally:
        db.close()


def test_note_api_requires_review_permission_and_keeps_value_revision(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    import app.main as main
    from app.database import get_db
    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, '_baseline_correction_item', lambda *args, **kwargs: (run, item))
    user = SimpleNamespace(username='备注运营', is_admin=False, role='viewer')
    main.app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    main.app.dependency_overrides[main.current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(main.app)
    try:
        url = f'/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/items/{item.id}/notes'
        body = {'text': '仅补充备注', 'idempotency_key': 'note-api-unique'}
        assert client.post(url, json=body).status_code == 403
        assert correction_notes(db, run_id=run.id, item_id=item.id) == []
        user.role = 'admin'; user.is_admin = True
        response = client.post(url, json=body)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()['notes'][0]['text'] == '仅补充备注'
        assert item.evaluation.review_revision == 3
        assert client.post(url, json={**body, 'text': ' '}).status_code == 422
    finally:
        main.app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_replay_factory_projection.py =====
"""字段纠偏重放到 Factory 投递节点时，必须像首次执行一样带上全部节点证据。

.137 复现：改 ``production_fields.category`` 后重放走到 ``downstream_projection``
（factory-result-v1），投递节点要求每个素材节点都在 upstream/skipped_upstream 里，
而重放只给了直接前驱 → ``factory_projection_incomplete``（422，禁止投递）。
"""
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.workflow_correction import replay_input_manifest
from app.workflow_node_executors import NodeExecutionContext, NodeExecutorError, execute_native_node


DOWNSTREAM = {
    "node_instance_id": "downstream",
    "kind": "downstream_projection",
    "config": {
        "connector_key": "logical.test.output",
        "mapping": {
            "schema_version": "factory-result-v1",
            "prompt_a_node_id": "prompt_a",
            "prompt_b_node_id": "prompt_b",
        },
    },
    "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
}
MANIFEST = {
    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
    "category_key": "model_3d_su",
    "nodes": [
        {"node_instance_id": "prompt_a", "kind": "prompt", "config": {}},
        {"node_instance_id": "prompt_b", "kind": "prompt", "config": {}},
        {"node_instance_id": "final_rule", "kind": "rule_module", "config": {}},
        DOWNSTREAM,
    ],
    "edges": [
        {"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"},
        {"from": "prompt_b", "to": "final_rule"},
        {"from": "final_rule", "to": "downstream"},
    ],
    "resource_policy": {"legacy_semantics": "documented_php_chain"},
}
ORDER = ["prompt_a", "prompt_b", "final_rule", "downstream"]


def _outputs(*, b_skipped: bool = False) -> dict:
    a = {
        "score": 99,
        "production_fields": {"title": "测试模型", "category": "植物3D模型素材（纠偏后）"},
        "model_3d_su_fields": {"watermark": ""},
    }
    outputs = {
        "prompt_a": {"node_kind": "prompt", "status": "succeeded", "output": deepcopy(a)},
        "prompt_b": {"node_kind": "prompt", "status": "succeeded", "output": {"aesthetic_score": 81}},
        "final_rule": {"node_kind": "rule_module", "status": "succeeded", "output": {"score": 73, "level": "L4"}},
    }
    if b_skipped:
        outputs["prompt_a"]["short_circuit_decision"] = {
            "schema_version": "short-circuit-decision-v1", "rule_key": "quality-l2",
            "target_level": "L2", "rule_version": "short-circuit-rule-v1",
            "skipped_nodes": ["prompt_b"],
            "evidence": {"priority": 90, "skip_roles": ["grading"], "matched_rule_keys": ["quality-l2"], "matched_conditions": []},
        }
        outputs["prompt_b"] = {"node_kind": "prompt", "status": "skipped", "output": {}}
    return outputs


def _project(input_manifest: dict) -> dict:
    return execute_native_node(NodeExecutionContext(
        run_snapshot={"workflow": {"manifest": MANIFEST}, "runtime_context": {"model_execution_mode": "real"}},
        node=DOWNSTREAM, input_manifest=input_manifest, raw_fixture_output={}, attempt_no=1, started_at=None,
    ))["output"]


def test_direct_predecessors_alone_are_rejected_by_the_factory_projection():
    """钉住 .137 的报错：只给直接前驱就是 422 禁止投递。"""
    outputs = _outputs()
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as exc:
        _project({"upstream": {"final_rule": outputs["final_rule"]}})
    assert exc.value.code == "factory_projection_incomplete"


def test_replay_hands_the_factory_projection_every_finished_node():
    outputs = _outputs()
    manifest = replay_input_manifest(
        manifest=MANIFEST, node=DOWNSTREAM, predecessors=["final_rule"],
        preceding=ORDER[:3], outputs=outputs,
    )
    assert set(manifest["upstream"]) == {"prompt_a", "prompt_b", "final_rule"}
    assert "skipped_upstream" not in manifest
    projected = _project(manifest)
    # 纠偏后的 A 事实要真的投出去，而不是被"素材流程尚未完整结束"挡住。
    assert projected["prompt_a_fields"]["production_fields"]["category"] == "植物3D模型素材（纠偏后）"
    assert projected["final_level"] == "L4" and projected["prompt_b_aesthetic_score"] == 81


def test_replay_explains_short_circuited_nodes_to_the_factory_projection():
    outputs = _outputs(b_skipped=True)
    manifest = replay_input_manifest(
        manifest=MANIFEST, node=DOWNSTREAM, predecessors=["final_rule"],
        preceding=ORDER[:3], outputs=outputs,
    )
    assert manifest["skipped_upstream"] == ["prompt_b"]
    assert set(manifest["upstream"]) == {"prompt_a", "final_rule"}
    _project(manifest)


def test_ordinary_nodes_still_replay_from_their_direct_predecessors_only():
    outputs = _outputs()
    rule = MANIFEST["nodes"][2]
    manifest = replay_input_manifest(
        manifest=MANIFEST, node=rule, predecessors=["prompt_b"],
        preceding=ORDER[:2], outputs=outputs,
    )
    assert set(manifest["upstream"]) == {"prompt_b"}, "普通节点的输入指纹不能因这次修复而漂移"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_schema_field_labels.py =====
"""冻结的严格输出 schema 只给字段名和类型，纠偏页要能显示成人话。

2026-09-10 运营实测反馈：3D/SU 的水印等 13 个专有字段一直在返回、也一直在纠偏
契约里，但页面上显示成裸英文键（watermark、qr_code……），运营认不出哪个是水印，
以为"字段没生成"。这里锁住中文名、可选值，以及 image_defects 在 3D/SU 的去留。
"""

import json
import pytest

from app.correction_contract import correction_fields_from_output_contract


def _obj(properties):
    """严格输出合同要求每层 object 都显式列出 required。"""

    return {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": properties,
        "required": sorted(properties),
    }


def _schema(properties):
    return {"mode": "json_schema", "name": "t", "schema": _obj(properties)}


MODEL_3D_SU_PROPS = {
    "production_fields": _obj({
        "title": {"type": "string"},
        "image_defects": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 400},
        "trait": {"type": "string"},
    }),
    "model_3d_su_fields": _obj({
        "watermark": {"type": "string"},
        "qr_code": {"type": "string"},
        "high_risk": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}},
    }),
}

INSPIRATION_PROPS = {
    "production_fields": _obj({
        "title": {"type": "string"},
        "image_defects": {"type": "string"},
    }),
}


def _by_key(fields):
    return {f["key"]: f for f in fields}


def test_model_3d_su_risk_fields_get_chinese_names_and_options():
    fields = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(_schema(MODEL_3D_SU_PROPS), {}))
    watermark = fields["model_3d_su_fields.watermark"]
    assert watermark["label"] == "水印"
    # 取值只有「空」或一句固定短语，做成可点选而不是让运营手打
    assert watermark["options"] == ["", "有水印"]
    assert watermark["type"] == "enum"
    assert "水印" in watermark["description"]

    qr = fields["model_3d_su_fields.qr_code"]
    assert qr["label"] == "二维码"
    assert qr["options"] == ["", "有二维码"]


def test_high_risk_stays_a_list_without_invented_options():
    """high_risk 是机器标记数组（如 cg_model），不能被压成两选一。"""

    fields = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(_schema(MODEL_3D_SU_PROPS), {}))
    high_risk = fields["model_3d_su_fields.high_risk"]
    assert high_risk["label"] == "高风险标记"
    assert high_risk["type"] == "array"
    assert "options" not in high_risk


def test_image_defects_is_dropped_only_for_model_3d_su():
    """3D/SU 的 image_defects 承载 CG 红线机器标记与 L4 事实描述，不是人工判定项。

    它在 3D/SU 里被摘掉（水印判定改看 model_3d_su_fields.watermark），
    但灵感图那条路线仍然要保留，不能被这条规则误伤。
    """

    su = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(_schema(MODEL_3D_SU_PROPS), {}))
    assert "production_fields.image_defects" not in su
    assert "production_fields.title" in su  # 同一节点的其他字段不受影响

    inspiration = _by_key(
        correction_fields_from_output_contract(_schema(INSPIRATION_PROPS), {})
    )
    assert "production_fields.image_defects" in inspiration
    assert inspiration["production_fields.image_defects"]["label"] == "图片缺陷"


def test_frozen_schema_wins_over_the_static_label_table():
    """提示词作者显式写了 title/description 时以冻结 schema 为准。

    静态表只补 schema 没写的部分，否则它会悄悄改掉某个版本的真实语义。
    """

    props = {
        "model_3d_su_fields": _obj({
            "watermark": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "本版专用水印口径",
                "description": "只认右下角平台水印",
            }
        })
    }
    fields = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(_schema(props), {}))
    watermark = fields["model_3d_su_fields.watermark"]
    assert watermark["label"] == "本版专用水印口径"
    assert watermark["description"] == "只认右下角平台水印"
    # 没写 enum 的仍然补可选值，方便点选
    assert watermark["options"] == ["", "有水印"]


def test_schema_enum_is_never_replaced_by_the_table():
    props = {
        "production_fields": _obj({
            "trait": {"type": "string", "enum": ["AI图", "实景照片"]},
        })
    }
    fields = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(_schema(props), {}))
    assert fields["production_fields.trait"]["options"] == ["AI图", "实景照片"]
    assert fields["production_fields.trait"]["label"] == "素材媒介"


def test_unknown_fields_keep_working_without_a_table_entry():
    props = {"production_fields": _obj({"brand_new": {"type": "string"}})}
    fields = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(_schema(props), {}))
    assert fields["production_fields.brand_new"]["label"] == "brand_new"


@pytest.mark.parametrize('schema,value,expected_type', [
    ({'type': 'boolean'}, True, 'boolean'),
    ({'type': 'array', 'items': {'type': 'string'}}, ['artpole'], 'array'),
])
def test_label_defaults_never_change_frozen_non_string_types(schema, value, expected_type):
    fields = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(
        _schema({'model_3d_su_fields': _obj({'brand_name': schema})}),
        {'model_3d_su_fields': {'brand_name': value}},
    ))
    field = fields['model_3d_su_fields.brand_name']
    assert field['type'] == expected_type
    assert 'options' not in field
    assert field['value'] == value


def test_all_risk_labels_and_historical_values_are_preserved():
    from app.model_3d_su_legacy_projection import RISK_FIELD_PHRASES
    props = {key: {'type': 'string'} for key in RISK_FIELD_PHRASES}
    fields = _by_key(correction_fields_from_output_contract(
        _schema({'model_3d_su_fields': _obj(props)}),
        {'model_3d_su_fields': {'brand_name': 'artpole'}},
    ))
    for key in RISK_FIELD_PHRASES:
        assert fields[f'model_3d_su_fields.{key}']['label'] != key
    assert fields['model_3d_su_fields.brand_name']['value'] == 'artpole'


def test_historical_display_overlay_preserves_identity_and_old_correction_fields():
    from copy import deepcopy
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.correction_view import _materialize_runtime_contract
    contract = {'nodes': [
        {'node_key': 'workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.watermark', 'label': 'watermark', 'type': 'text', 'description': '', 'metadata': {}},
        {'node_key': 'workflow.prompt_a.production_fields.image_defects', 'label': 'image_defects', 'type': 'text', 'metadata': {}},
        {'node_key': 'workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.brand_name', 'label': '本版品牌定义', 'type': 'text', 'description': '作者定义', 'metadata': {}},
    ]}
    before = deepcopy(contract)
    result = _materialize_runtime_contract(contract, SimpleNamespace())
    assert result['nodes'][0]['label'] == '水印'
    assert result['nodes'][0]['type'] == 'text'
    assert 'options' not in result['nodes'][0]
    assert result['nodes'][1]['node_key'].endswith('image_defects')
    assert result['nodes'][2]['label'] == '本版品牌定义'
    assert result['nodes'][2]['description'] == '作者定义'
    assert contract == before
    assert _materialize_runtime_contract(result, SimpleNamespace()) == result


def test_historical_3dsu_b_labels_do_not_disable_entire_correction_contract():
    from copy import deepcopy
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.correction_contract import correction_contract_hash
    from app.correction_view import build_correction_view, submit_correction_nodes, CorrectionViewError
    node = {'node_key':'workflow.prompt_b.aesthetic_score', 'path':'workflow.prompt_b.output.aesthetic_score',
        'layer':'B', 'label':'aesthetic_score', 'description':'美感初始分', 'type':'number',
        'minimum':0, 'maximum':100, 'required':True, 'semantic_version':'1',
        'compatibility_key':'score-v1', 'evidence':{'description':'可见依据'},
        'metadata':{'workflow_kind':'prompt','node_instance_id':'prompt_b','editable':True}}
    contract = {'contract_version':'1','category_key':'model_3d_su','nodes':[node]}
    contract['contract_hash'] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    before = deepcopy(contract)
    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(review_revision=0, correction_history_json='[]',
        workflow_node_outputs_json=canonical_json({'prompt_b':{'status':'succeeded',
            'node_instance_id':'prompt_b','output':{'aesthetic_score':88}}}),
        precheck_json='{}', aesthetic_json='{}', scoring_json='{}', reviews=[])
    run = SimpleNamespace(id=1, category_key='model_3d_su', status='running',
        correction_contract_json=canonical_json(contract), correction_contract_hash=contract['contract_hash'],
        execution_snapshot_json='{}')
    item = SimpleNamespace(id=2, run_id=1, evaluation_id=3, evaluation=evaluation, result_snapshot_json='{}')
    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)
    assert view['read_only'] is False
    assert view['nodes'][0]['label'] == '美感初始分'
    assert view['contract']['contract_hash'] == contract['contract_hash']
    assert json.loads(run.correction_contract_json) == before
    # Submission must pass the same presentation validation, retaining original identity.
    with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError) as caught:
        submit_correction_nodes(None, run=run, item=item, contract_hash=contract['contract_hash'],
            nodes=[], review_revision=0, idempotency_key='test', actor='test')
    assert caught.value.code == 'CORRECTION_NODES_EMPTY'
    contract['nodes'][0]['type'] = 'invalid-type'
    contract['contract_hash'] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    run.correction_contract_json = canonical_json(contract)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract['contract_hash']
    assert build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)['read_only'] is True

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_table_view.py =====
from copy import deepcopy

from app.correction_view import table_correction_view


def test_table_projection_keeps_values_editing_rules_and_frozen_identity():
    view = {
        'contract': {'contract_hash': 'frozen'}, 'review_revision': 7,
        'notes': [{'id': 1, 'text': 'keep'}], 'read_only': False,
        'nodes': [
            {'node_key': 'edit', 'type': 'array', 'human_value': None,
             'current_value': False, 'model_value': 0, 'minItems': 1,
             'evidence_entries': [{'text': 'human evidence'}],
             'metadata': {'field_identity': {'field_path': 'x'},
                          'output_array_item_schema': {'type': 'number'},
                          'paid_rerun_required': True, 'editable': True,
                          'output_contract': {'huge': 'x' * 10000}},
             'execution_evidence': {'huge': 'y' * 10000}},
            {'node_key': 'readonly', 'read_only': True},
            {'node_key': 'disabled', 'metadata': {'editable': False}},
        ],
    }
    before = deepcopy(view)
    result = table_correction_view(view)
    assert view == before
    assert result['contract'] == view['contract']
    assert result['review_revision'] == 7 and result['notes'] == view['notes']
    assert len(result['nodes']) == 1
    node = result['nodes'][0]
    assert node['human_value'] is None and node['current_value'] is False
    assert node['model_value'] == 0 and node['minItems'] == 1
    assert node['evidence_entries'] == view['nodes'][0]['evidence_entries']
    assert node['metadata']['paid_rerun_required'] is True
    assert node['metadata']['output_array_item_schema'] == {'type': 'number'}
    assert 'output_contract' not in node['metadata']
    assert 'execution_evidence' not in node

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_view.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from copy import deepcopy
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.correction_contract import (
    correction_contract_hash,
    freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot,
)
from app.correction_view import (
    CorrectionViewError,
    build_correction_nodes,
    build_correction_view,
    submit_correction_nodes,
)
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.dimension_deduction_bridge import empty_deduction_output
from app.evaluation_v3_pipeline import recompute_qualified_v3
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    build_inspiration_classification_map,
    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import (
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    EvaluationResult,
    SampleSet,
    SampleSetItem,
)
from app.worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring


def _node(
    node_key: str,
    *,
    layer: str = "A",
    path: str | None = None,
    node_type: str = "text",
    semantic_version: str = "1",
    compatibility_key: str | None = None,
    required_evidence: bool = False,
) -> dict:
    node = {
        "node_key": node_key,
        "layer": layer,
        "path": path or node_key,
        "order": 1,
        "label": f"节点{node_key}",
        "description": f"用于纠正节点{node_key}的人工判断",
        "type": node_type,
        "semantic_version": semantic_version,
        "compatibility_key": compatibility_key or node_key,
        "required": True,
        "evidence": {
            "description": f"请提供节点{node_key}的图片证据",
            "required": required_evidence,
        },
    }
    if node_type == "enum":
        node["options"] = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]
    if layer == "V3":
        node["recompute_ref"] = "evaluation_v3_pipeline.recompute_qualified_v3"
        node["steps"] = ["读取冻结规则", "服务端重新计算", "返回最终等级"]
    return node


def _contract(*nodes: dict, version: str = "2") -> dict:
    contract = {
        "contract_version": version,
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "nodes": list(nodes),
    }
    contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    return contract


def test_build_nodes_inherits_only_compatible_values_and_omits_deleted_nodes() -> None:
    stable = _node("call_a.title", path="call_a.title")
    changed = _node(
        "call_b.composition",
        layer="B",
        path="dimension.composition.hit_rules",
        node_type="list",
        semantic_version="2",
    )
    added = _node("call_a.style", path="call_a.style")
    final = _node(
        "v3.final_level",
        layer="V3",
        path="final_level",
        node_type="enum",
    )
    final["metadata"] = {
        "expression": "score >= hidden_threshold",
        "ui": {"hint": "只读说明", "code": "return hidden_rule"},
    }
    previous_values = {
        "call_a.title": {
            **stable,
            "human_value": "现代住宅",
            "reason": "主体明确",
            "evidence": [{"text": "画面主体为住宅"}],
        },
        "call_b.composition": {
            **changed,
            "semantic_version": "1",
            "human_value": ["rule-old"],
        },
        "deleted.node": {
            **_node("deleted.node"),
            "human_value": "旧值",
        },
    }

    nodes = build_correction_nodes(
        _contract(stable, changed, added, final),
        model_values={
            "call_a.title": "模型标题",
            "call_a.style": "模型风格",
            "v3.final_level": "L2",
        },
        human_values={},
        previous_values=previous_values,
    )

    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in nodes}
    assert set(by_key) == {
        "call_a.title",
        "call_b.composition",
        "call_a.style",
        "v3.final_level",
    }
    assert by_key["call_a.title"]["human_value"] == "现代住宅"
    assert by_key["call_a.title"]["inheritance"]["status"] == "inherited"
    assert by_key["call_b.composition"]["inheritance"]["status"] == "changed"
    assert "human_value" not in by_key["call_b.composition"]
    assert by_key["call_a.style"]["inheritance"]["status"] == "new"
    assert by_key["v3.final_level"]["steps"] == [
        "读取冻结规则",
        "服务端重新计算",
        "返回最终等级",
    ]
    assert "rule" not in by_key["v3.final_level"]
    assert "expression" not in by_key["v3.final_level"]["metadata"]
    assert by_key["v3.final_level"]["metadata"]["ui"] == {
        "hint": "只读说明"
    }


def test_read_only_nodes_use_frozen_value_and_cannot_be_submitted() -> None:
    threshold_node = _node(
        "v3.level_thresholds",
        layer="V3",
        path="scoring.level_thresholds",
        node_type="list",
    )
    threshold_node["metadata"] = {
        "editable": False,
        "frozen_value": [
            {"min_score": 90, "level": "L1"},
            {"min_score": 75, "level": "L2"},
        ],
    }
    contract = _contract(threshold_node)
    nodes = build_correction_nodes(
        contract,
        model_values={},
        current_values={},
        human_values={},
        previous_values={},
    )

    assert nodes[0]["model_value"] == threshold_node["metadata"]["frozen_value"]
    assert nodes[0]["current_value"] == threshold_node["metadata"]["frozen_value"]
    assert nodes[0]["editable"] is False

    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract["contract_hash"]
    with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError) as exc_info:
        submit_correction_nodes(
            db,
            run=run,
            item=item,
            contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
            nodes=[
                {
                    "node_key": "v3.level_thresholds",
                    "human_value": threshold_node["metadata"]["frozen_value"],
                    "reason": "阈值需要修改",
                    "evidence": [{"text": "人工判断"}],
                }
            ],
            review_revision=3,
            idempotency_key="read-only-threshold-0001",
            actor="运营乙",
        )
    assert exc_info.value.code == "CORRECTION_NODE_READ_ONLY"
    db.close()


def test_build_view_reads_only_the_run_frozen_contract() -> None:
    old_node = _node("call_a.title", path="call_a.title")
    old_contract = _contract(old_node, version="1")
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=17,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(old_contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=old_contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "correction_contract": old_contract,
                "active_contract_that_must_not_be_read": _contract(
                    _node("new.active.node")
                ),
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=23,
        run_id=17,
        asset_id=101,
        evaluation_id=None,
        evaluation=None,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"stage_a": {"production_fields": {"title": "冻结标题"}}},
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )

    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)

    assert view["contract"]["contract_hash"] == old_contract["contract_hash"]
    assert view["snapshot_status"] == "frozen"
    assert [node["node_key"] for node in view["nodes"]] == ["call_a.title"]
    assert view["nodes"][0]["model_value"] == "冻结标题"

def _submission_fixture() -> tuple[Session, SimpleNamespace, SimpleNamespace, dict]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=101,
        job_id=201,
        precheck_json=json.dumps(
            {"production_fields": {"title": "模型标题"}}, ensure_ascii=False
        ),
        aesthetic_json="{}",
        scoring_json=json.dumps({"score": 70, "level": "L2"}),
        correction_history_json=json.dumps(
            [
                {
                    "correction_key": "older-event",
                    "node_type": "call_a_field",
                    "node_path": "call_a.title",
                    "old_value": "更早标题",
                    "new_value": "模型标题",
                    "evidence": [],
                    "reason": "既有历史",
                    "corrector": "运营甲",
                    "corrected_at": "2026-08-18T00:00:00+00:00",
                    "downstream_recomputed": False,
                }
            ],
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        raw_response_a='{"immutable":"provider response"}',
        raw_response_b=None,
        score=70,
        level="L2",
        confidence=0.8,
        needs_review=False,
        review_revision=3,
        model_id="fake-model",
        prompt_a_version="a-v1",
        prompt_b_version="b-v1",
        rubric_version="r-v1",
        engine_version="e-v1",
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.flush()
    contract_node = _node(
        "call_a.title",
        path="call_a.title",
        required_evidence=True,
    )
    contract_node["metadata"] = {"node_type": "call_a_field"}
    contract = _contract(contract_node)
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=17,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json="{}",
    )
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=23,
        run_id=17,
        asset_id=101,
        evaluation_id=result.id,
        evaluation=result,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"stage_a": {"production_fields": {"title": "模型标题"}}},
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )
    return db, run, item, contract


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("override", "code"),
    [
        ({"contract_hash": "0" * 64}, "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_STALE"),
        (
            {
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_key": "outside.contract",
                        "human_value": "值",
                        "reason": "理由",
                        "evidence": [{"text": "证据"}],
                    }
                ]
            },
            "CORRECTION_NODE_UNKNOWN",
        ),
        ({"review_revision": 2}, "CORRECTION_REVIEW_STALE"),
        (
            {
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_key": "call_a.title",
                        "human_value": "新标题",
                        "reason": "理由",
                        "evidence": [],
                    }
                ]
            },
            "CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_submit_nodes_fails_closed(override: dict, code: str) -> None:
    db, run, item, contract = _submission_fixture()
    request = {
        "contract_hash": contract["contract_hash"],
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_key": "call_a.title",
                "human_value": "新标题",
                "reason": "主体识别错误",
                "evidence": [{"text": "主体是现代住宅"}],
            }
        ],
        "review_revision": 3,
        "idempotency_key": "submission-0001",
        **override,
    }

    with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError) as exc_info:
        submit_correction_nodes(db, run=run, item=item, actor="运营乙", **request)

    assert exc_info.value.code == code
    assert item.evaluation.review_revision == 3
    assert len(json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)) == 1
    db.close()


def test_submit_rejects_invalid_enum_value() -> None:
    db, run, item, _contract_value = _submission_fixture()
    enum_node = _node(
        "call_a.grade",
        path="call_a.grade",
        node_type="enum",
        required_evidence=True,
    )
    enum_node["metadata"] = {"node_type": "call_a_field"}
    contract = _contract(enum_node)
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract["contract_hash"]

    with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError) as exc_info:
        submit_correction_nodes(
            db,
            run=run,
            item=item,
            contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
            nodes=[
                {
                    "node_key": "call_a.grade",
                    "human_value": "L9",
                    "reason": "等级需要修正",
                    "evidence": [{"text": "不符合当前等级"}],
                }
            ],
            review_revision=3,
            idempotency_key="submission-enum",
            actor="运营乙",
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "CORRECTION_NODE_VALUE_INVALID"
    db.close()


def test_valid_submission_appends_history_preserves_raw_response_and_is_idempotent() -> None:
    db, run, item, contract = _submission_fixture()
    raw_before = item.evaluation.raw_response_a
    old_history = deepcopy(json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json))
    request = {
        "contract_hash": contract["contract_hash"],
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_key": "call_a.title",
                "human_value": "现代住宅",
                "reason": "主体是住宅而不是泛空间",
                "evidence": [{"text": "画面中央为现代住宅立面"}],
            }
        ],
        "review_revision": 3,
        "idempotency_key": "submission-valid-0001",
        "actor": "运营乙",
    }

    view = submit_correction_nodes(db, run=run, item=item, **request)

    history = json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)
    assert history[:1] == old_history
    assert len(history) == 2
    assert history[-1]["node_key"] == "call_a.title"
    assert history[-1]["contract_hash"] == contract["contract_hash"]
    assert history[-1]["idempotency_key"] == "submission-valid-0001"
    assert item.evaluation.raw_response_a == raw_before
    assert item.evaluation.review_revision == 4
    assert view["review_revision"] == 4
    assert view["nodes"][0]["human_value"] == "现代住宅"

    replay = submit_correction_nodes(
        db,
        run=run,
        item=item,
        review_revision=3,
        **{key: value for key, value in request.items() if key != "review_revision"},
    )

    assert replay["idempotent_replay"] is True
    assert len(json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)) == 2
    db.close()


def test_baseline_final_level_correction_becomes_next_run_human_truth() -> None:
    db, run, item, _contract_value = _submission_fixture()
    final_node = _node(
        "v3.final_level",
        layer="V3",
        path="scoring.level",
        node_type="enum",
        required_evidence=True,
    )
    final_node["metadata"] = {"node_type": "final_level"}
    contract = _contract(final_node)
    run.baseline_set_id = 88
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract["contract_hash"]
    item.evaluation.scoring_json = json.dumps(
        {"score": 70, "level": "L2", "v3_context": {}},
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )

    submit_correction_nodes(
        db,
        run=run,
        item=item,
        contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        nodes=[
            {
                "node_key": "v3.final_level",
                "human_value": "L1",
                "reason": "人工确认应升档",
                "evidence": [{"text": "整体完成度达到推荐档"}],
            }
        ],
        review_revision=3,
        idempotency_key="baseline-final-level-0001",
        actor="运营乙",
    )

    golden = db.scalar(
        select(SampleSet).where(
            SampleSet.name == "系统黄金集·inspiration_image"
        )
    )
    assert golden is not None
    truth = db.scalar(
        select(SampleSetItem).where(
            SampleSetItem.sample_set_id == golden.id,
            SampleSetItem.asset_id == item.asset_id,
        )
    )
    assert truth is not None
    assert truth.expected_level == "L1"
    assert json.loads(truth.truth_json)["corrected_level"] == "L1"
    db.close()


def test_reused_idempotency_key_with_different_payload_is_rejected() -> None:
    db, run, item, contract = _submission_fixture()
    original = {
        "contract_hash": contract["contract_hash"],
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_key": "call_a.title",
                "human_value": "现代住宅",
                "reason": "主体是住宅",
                "evidence": [{"text": "住宅立面"}],
            }
        ],
        "review_revision": 3,
        "idempotency_key": "submission-conflict",
        "actor": "运营乙",
    }
    submit_correction_nodes(db, run=run, item=item, **original)
    changed = deepcopy(original)
    changed["nodes"][0]["human_value"] = "办公空间"

    with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError) as exc_info:
        submit_correction_nodes(db, run=run, item=item, **changed)

    assert exc_info.value.code == "CORRECTION_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT"
    db.close()


def test_baseline_correction_view_routes_return_frozen_view_and_stable_errors() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=101,
        job_id=201,
        precheck_json=json.dumps(
            {"production_fields": {"title": "模型标题"}}, ensure_ascii=False
        ),
        aesthetic_json="{}",
        scoring_json=json.dumps({"score": 70, "level": "L2"}),
        correction_history_json="[]",
        raw_response_a='{"immutable":true}',
        score=70,
        level="L2",
        confidence=0.8,
        needs_review=False,
        review_revision=0,
        model_id="fake-model",
        prompt_a_version="a-v1",
        prompt_b_version="b-v1",
        rubric_version="r-v1",
        engine_version="e-v1",
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.flush()
    node = _node("call_a.title", path="call_a.title", required_evidence=True)
    node["metadata"] = {"node_type": "call_a_field"}
    contract = _contract(node)
    run = BaselineRegressionRun(
        baseline_set_id=501,
        sequence_no=1,
        strategy_bundle_id=601,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
        execution_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"correction_contract": contract}, ensure_ascii=False
        ),
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        baseline_set_fingerprint="f" * 64,
        status="completed",
        total=1,
        completed=1,
        valid_predictions=1,
        failed=0,
        metrics_json="{}",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    item = BaselineRegressionItem(
        run_id=run.id,
        baseline_set_item_id=701,
        asset_id=101,
        expected_level="L1",
        evaluation_id=result.id,
        status="completed",
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "predicted_level": "L2",
                "stage_a": {"production_fields": {"title": "模型标题"}},
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )
    db.add(item)
    db.commit()
    user = SimpleNamespace(username="运营乙", is_admin=True, role="admin")
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        view_response = client.get(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/items/{item.id}/correction-view"
        )
        assert view_response.status_code == 200
        assert view_response.json()["contract"]["contract_hash"] == contract[
            "contract_hash"
        ]
        batch = client.get(f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/correction-views", params={"item_ids": item.id})
        assert batch.status_code == 200 and batch.json()["items"] == [view_response.json()]
        from app.correction_view import table_correction_view
        compact = client.get(f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/correction-views", params={"item_ids": item.id, "presentation": "table"})
        assert compact.json()["items"] == [table_correction_view(view_response.json())]
        compact_single = client.get(f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/items/{item.id}/correction-view?presentation=table")
        assert compact_single.json() == compact.json()["items"][0]
        assert client.get(f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/correction-views", params={"item_ids": item.id + 99999}).status_code == 404
        assert client.get(f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/correction-views", params=[("item_ids", item.id)] * 51).status_code == 422

        stale = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/items/{item.id}/corrections?presentation=table",
            json={
                "contract_hash": "0" * 64,
                "review_revision": 0,
                "idempotency_key": "api-stale-0001",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_key": "call_a.title",
                        "human_value": "现代住宅",
                        "reason": "主体识别错误",
                        "evidence": [{"text": "主体为住宅"}],
                    }
                ],
            },
        )
        assert stale.status_code == 409
        assert stale.json()["detail"]["code"] == "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_STALE"

        saved = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/items/{item.id}/corrections?presentation=table",
            json={
                "contract_hash": contract["contract_hash"],
                "review_revision": 0,
                "idempotency_key": "api-valid-0001",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_key": "call_a.title",
                        "human_value": "现代住宅",
                        "reason": "主体识别错误",
                        "evidence": [{"text": "主体为住宅"}],
                    }
                ],
            },
        )
        assert saved.status_code == 200
        assert saved.json()["review_revision"] == 1
        assert saved.json()["nodes"][0]["human_value"] == "现代住宅"
        full_after = client.get(f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/items/{item.id}/correction-view").json()
        assert saved.json() == table_correction_view(full_after)
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_v3_rule_submission_uses_authoritative_recompute_and_preserves_raw_payload() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    context = {
        "contract": build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        "classification_map": build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        "subcategory_dimensions": build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions(),
        "config_revision": 3,
    }
    precheck = {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "in_scope",
            "primary_category": "建筑设计",
            "primary_confidence": 0.96,
        },
        "production_fields": {"reason": [], "trait": "实景照片"},
    }
    config = context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    dimension_output = empty_deduction_output(config)
    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=dimension_output,
    )
    scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=precheck)
    scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
    scoring["v3_context"] = context
    first_dimension = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"][0]
    first_rule = first_dimension["deduction_rules"][0]
    node_key = f"v3.{first_dimension['key']}.{first_rule['rule_id']}"
    path = (
        f"dimension.{first_dimension['key']}.hit_rules.{first_rule['rule_id']}"
    )
    contract_node = _node(
        node_key,
        layer="V3",
        path=path,
        node_type="rule_hit",
        required_evidence=True,
    )
    contract_node["metadata"] = {"node_type": "dimension_rule"}
    contract = _contract(contract_node)
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=101,
        job_id=201,
        precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False),
        aesthetic_json=json.dumps(dimension_output, ensure_ascii=False),
        scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_history_json="[]",
        raw_response_a='{"immutable":"provider payload"}',
        raw_response_b='{"immutable":"dimension payload"}',
        score=scoring["score"],
        level=scoring["level"],
        confidence=scoring["confidence"],
        needs_review=False,
        review_revision=0,
        model_id="fake-model",
        prompt_a_version="a-v1",
        prompt_b_version="b-v1",
        rubric_version="r-v1",
        engine_version="e-v1",
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.flush()
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=17,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json="{}",
    )
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=23,
        run_id=17,
        asset_id=101,
        evaluation_id=result.id,
        evaluation=result,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "predicted_level": result.level,
                "authoritative_score": result.score,
                "stage_a": precheck,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )
    new_hit = {
        "rule_id": first_rule["rule_id"],
        "confidence": "medium",
        "evidence": "主体明显偏移，左侧大面积空置",
    }
    expected_dimension_output = deepcopy(dimension_output)
    expected_dimension_output["dimensions"][first_dimension["key"]][
        "hit_rules"
    ] = [new_hit]
    expected_aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=deepcopy(precheck),
        dimension_output=expected_dimension_output,
    )
    expected_scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(
        expected_aggregate, precheck=precheck
    )
    raw_a_before = result.raw_response_a
    raw_b_before = result.raw_response_b

    view = submit_correction_nodes(
        db,
        run=run,
        item=item,
        contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        nodes=[
            {
                "node_key": node_key,
                "human_value": new_hit,
                "reason": "人工确认该扣分规则命中",
                "evidence": [
                    {
                        "rule_id": first_rule["rule_id"],
                        "new_confidence": "medium",
                        "new_evidence": new_hit["evidence"],
                    }
                ],
            }
        ],
        review_revision=0,
        idempotency_key="v3-recompute-0001",
        actor="运营乙",
    )

    assert result.score == expected_scoring["score"]
    assert result.level == expected_scoring["level"]
    assert result.raw_response_a == raw_a_before
    assert result.raw_response_b == raw_b_before
    assert view["nodes"][0]["current_value"] == new_hit
    assert view["nodes"][0]["human_value"] == new_hit
    db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def test_workflow_prompt_node_correction_is_append_only_and_self_describing() -> None:
    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    workflow_snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "version": "7",
            "canonical_hash": "b" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v2",
                "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "key": "prompt-c",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "display_name": "调用提示词 C",
                        "config": {
                            "prompt_ref": "prompt:C@workflow-v1",
                            "model_registry_id": 8,
                            "max_tokens": 4096,
                            "thinking_level": "medium",
                        },
                    }
                ],
                "edges": [],
            },
        },
        "prompts": [
            {
                "reference": "prompt:C@workflow-v1",
                "prompt_id": 21,
                "version": "workflow-v1",
                "content_sha256": "c" * 64,
            }
        ],
        "models": [
            {
                "model_registry_id": 8,
                "name": "运营评测模型",
                "model_id": "model-8",
            }
        ],
        "rules": [],
    }
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(workflow_snapshot)
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract["contract_hash"]
    item.evaluation.correction_history_json = json.dumps(
        [
            {
                "schema_version": "contract-node-correction-v1",
                "node_key": "workflow.prompt-c",
                "workflow_node_key": "prompt-c",
                "run_id": run.id,
                "contract_hash": "0" * 64,
                "new_value": {"level": "L5", "reason": "旧合同纠偏"},
                "reason": "不应继承到当前合同",
            }
        ],
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )
    item.result_snapshot_json = json.dumps(
        {
            "workflow_node_outputs": {
                "prompt-c": {"output": {"level": "L2", "reason": "模型判断"}}
            }
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )

    initial = build_correction_view(db, run=run, item=item)
    assert initial["nodes"][0]["model_value"] == {
        "level": "L2",
        "reason": "模型判断",
    }
    assert initial["nodes"][0].get("human_value") is None

    corrected = submit_correction_nodes(
        db,
        run=run,
        item=item,
        contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        nodes=[
            {
                "node_key": "workflow.prompt-c",
                "human_value": {"level": "L3", "reason": "人工复核"},
                "reason": "模型忽略了明显瑕疵",
                "evidence": [{"text": "画面右下角存在破面"}],
            }
        ],
        review_revision=3,
        idempotency_key="workflow-node-correction-0001",
        actor="运营乙",
    )

    assert corrected["nodes"][0]["human_value"] == {
        "level": "L3",
        "reason": "人工复核",
    }
    history = json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)
    assert history[-1]["node_key"] == "workflow.prompt-c"
    assert history[-1]["schema_version"] == "contract-node-correction-v1"
    assert history[-1]["workflow_node_key"] == "prompt-c"
    assert history[-1]["run_id"] == run.id
    assert history[-1]["workflow_version_id"] is None
    assert history[-1]["frozen_asset_identity"]["prompt_version"]["prompt_id"] == 21
    db.close()


def test_workflow_v3_prompt_without_field_schema_reads_whole_runtime_output() -> None:
    workflow_snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 81,
            "workflow_version_id": 91,
            "version": "12",
            "canonical_hash": "e" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "display_name": "提示词 C",
                        "config": {
                            "prompt_version_id": 31,
                            "model_registry_id": 7,
                            "max_tokens": 2048,
                            "thinking_level": "high",
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    }
                ],
                "edges": [],
            },
        }
    }
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(workflow_snapshot)
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=17,
        category_key="space_image",
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json="{}",
    )
    expected = {
        "space": "室内空间",
        "style": "现代",
        "material": "木材, 石材",
    }
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=23,
        run_id=17,
        asset_id=101,
        evaluation_id=None,
        evaluation=None,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "workflow_node_outputs": {
                    "prompt_c": {
                        "status": "succeeded",
                        "output": {
                            "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                            "output": expected,
                        },
                    }
                }
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )

    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)

    prompt_node = next(
        node
        for node in view["nodes"]
        if node["node_key"] == "workflow.prompt_c.output"
    )
    assert prompt_node["path"] == "workflow.prompt_c.output"
    assert prompt_node["model_value"] == expected

def test_workflow_v3_view_reads_nested_runtime_outputs_per_business_field() -> None:
    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    contract = _workflow_v3_contract_for_instance_corrections()
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract["contract_hash"]
    item.result_snapshot_json = json.dumps(
        {
            "workflow_node_outputs": {
                "prompt_c": {
                    "status": "succeeded",
                    "output": {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                        "component_version_id": 31,
                        "output": {
                            "category_name": "客厅空间",
                            "scene_tags": ["现代", "自然采光"],
                        },
                    },
                },
                "redline_first": {
                    "status": "succeeded",
                    "output": {
                        "node_instance_id": "redline_first",
                        "component_version_id": 41,
                        "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                        "output": {
                            "hit": True,
                            "matched_rules": ["copyright_risk"],
                            "level_cap": "L4",
                            "reason": "疑似版权水印",
                        },
                    },
                },
                "redline_second": {
                    "status": "succeeded",
                    "output": {
                        "node_instance_id": "redline_second",
                        "component_version_id": 42,
                        "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                        "output": {
                            "hit": False,
                            "matched_rules": [],
                            "level_cap": "L1",
                            "reason": "复核后未命中",
                        },
                    },
                },
            }
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )

    view = build_correction_view(db, run=run, item=item)
    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in view["nodes"]}

    assert by_key["workflow.prompt_c.category_name"]["model_value"] == "客厅空间"
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt_c.scene_tags"]["model_value"] == [
        "现代",
        "自然采光",
    ]
    assert by_key["workflow.redline_first.hit"]["model_value"] is True
    assert by_key["workflow.redline_second.hit"]["model_value"] is False
    db.close()


def test_workflow_v3_rule_nodes_read_authoritative_scoring_not_fixture_outputs() -> None:
    """The correction form must show the finalized business result.

    Rule-module attempts deliberately retain deterministic runtime evidence.
    Native level workflows are finalized by the authoritative scoring engine,
    so exposing the fixture output here would ask an operator to correct values
    that were never used for the stored score and level.
    """

    workflow_snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 82,
            "workflow_version_id": 92,
            "version": "33",
            "canonical_hash": "f" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "model_3d_su",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "redline",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "准入与红线",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 50,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "track_router",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "赛道区分",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "track_routing",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 51,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "dimension_rules",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "维度扣分",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "dimension_scoring",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 52,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "media_rules",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "媒介调整",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "media_adjustment",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 54,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "quality_rules",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "质量上限与例外",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "quality_cap_exception",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 55,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "anchor_rules",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "锚点校准",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 56,

                    },
                ],
                "edges": [
                    {"from": "redline", "to": "track_router"},
                    {"from": "track_router", "to": "dimension_rules"},
                    {"from": "dimension_rules", "to": "media_rules"},
                    {"from": "media_rules", "to": "quality_rules"},
                    {"from": "quality_rules", "to": "anchor_rules"},
                    {"from": "anchor_rules", "to": "level_matcher"},
                ],
            },
        }
    }
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(workflow_snapshot)
    fixture_outputs = {
        "prompt_a": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {
                "classification": {
                    "primary_confidence": 0.93,
                    "evidence": ["主体为完整建筑空间"],
                },
                "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "normal"},
                "production_fields": {"trait": "3D数字效果图"},
            },
        },
        "redline": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {
                "hit": True,
                "matched_rules": ["fixture"],
                "level_cap": "L5",
                "reason": "fixture",
            },
        },
        "track_router": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {"track_key": "", "confidence": "low", "reason": "fixture"},
        },
        "dimension_rules": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {"dimensions": {}, "total_score": 0, "reason": "fixture"},
        },
        "media_rules": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {
                "media_type": "",
                "score_adjustment": 0,
                "reason": "fixture",
            },
        },
        "quality_rules": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {
                "quality_grade": "",
                "score_cap": 0,

                "reason": "fixture",
