            "output": {"score": 0, "level": "", "reason": "fixture"},
        },
    }
    dimensions = {
        "composition": {
            "deduction": 0,
            "evidence": ["主体关系清晰"],
        }
    }
    scoring = {
        "hard_reject": False,
        "hit_rules": [],
        "track_key": "space_building",
        "score": 85,
        "level": "L1",
        "confidence": 0.93,
        "media_key": "render_3d",
        "media_penalty": -5,
        "hard_defect_action": {"cap_to": 78},
        "caps": [
            {"cap": "hard_defect_exemption", "reason": "已应用有证据例外"}
        ],
        "dimension_deduction_output": {
            "aesthetic_score": 85,
            "dimensions": dimensions,
        },
        "foundation_sha256": "8" * 64,
        "foundation_before_rules": 88,
        "foundation_after_rules": 84,
        "steps": [
            {
                "step": "redline",
                "score_after": None,
                "note": "无红线命中，进入分数计算流程",
            },
            {
                "step": "track",
                "score_after": 85,
                "note": "赛道 space_building：调用B美感基础分 85",
            },
            {
                "step": "dimension_rule_deduction",
                "score_after": 85,
                "note": "维度扣分（规则命中）：应用扣分 0",
            },
            {
                "step": "media",
                "score_after": 80,
                "note": "媒介类型 render_3d，固定扣分 -5",
            },
            {
                "step": "veto",
                "score_after": 78,
                "note": "硬伤封顶至 78",
            },
            {
                "step": "level",
                "score_after": 85,
                "note": "分数 85 → L1",
            },
        ],
    }
    precheck = {
        "classification": {
            "primary_confidence": 0.93,
            "evidence": ["主体为完整建筑空间"],
        }
    }
    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(
        review_revision=0,
        correction_history_json="[]",
        workflow_node_outputs_json=json.dumps(fixture_outputs, ensure_ascii=False),
        precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False),
        aesthetic_json="{}",
        scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
        score=85,
        level="L1",
        reviews=[],
    )
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=18,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json="{}",
    )
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=24,
        run_id=18,
        asset_id=102,
        evaluation_id=99,
        evaluation=evaluation,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "predicted_level": "L1",
                "authoritative_score": 85,
                "stage_a": precheck,
                "scoring": scoring,
                "workflow_node_outputs": fixture_outputs,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )

    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)
    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in view["nodes"]}

    assert by_key["workflow.redline.hit"]["current_value"] is False
    assert by_key["workflow.redline.matched_rules"]["current_value"] == []
    assert by_key["workflow.track_router.track_key"]["current_value"] == "space_building"
    assert by_key["workflow.track_router.confidence"]["current_value"] == "high"
    assert by_key["workflow.dimension_rules.dimensions"]["current_value"] == dimensions
    assert by_key["workflow.dimension_rules.total_score"]["current_value"] == 85
    assert by_key["workflow.media_rules.media_type"]["current_value"] == "render_3d"
    assert by_key["workflow.media_rules.score_adjustment"]["current_value"] == -5
    assert by_key["workflow.quality_rules.quality_grade"]["current_value"] == "normal"
    assert by_key["workflow.quality_rules.score_cap"]["current_value"] == 78
    assert by_key["workflow.quality_rules.exception_applied"]["current_value"] is True
    assert by_key["workflow.anchor_rules.anchor_id"]["current_value"] == "8" * 64
    assert by_key["workflow.anchor_rules.calibrated_score"]["current_value"] == 84
    assert by_key["workflow.level_matcher.score"]["current_value"] == 85
    assert by_key["workflow.level_matcher.level"]["current_value"] == "L1"


def test_workflow_v3_rule_nodes_support_inspiration_foundation_shape() -> None:
    workflow_snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 84,
            "workflow_version_id": 94,
            "version": "59",
            "canonical_hash": "7" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "media",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "媒介调整",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "media_adjustment",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 61,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "quality",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "质量上限与例外",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "quality_cap_exception",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 62,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "dimensions",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "维度扣分",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "dimension_scoring",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 63,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "anchor",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "display_name": "锚点校准",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 64,
                        },
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                ],
                "edges": [
                    {"from": "media", "to": "quality"},
                    {"from": "quality", "to": "dimensions"},
                    {"from": "dimensions", "to": "anchor"},
                ],
            },
        }
    }
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(workflow_snapshot)
    fixture_outputs = {
        "prompt_a": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {
                "track_confidence": 0.95,
                "media_type": "real_photo",
                "trait": "实景照片",
            },
        },
        **{
            node_id: {
                "status": "succeeded",
                "output": {
                    "dimensions": {},
                    "total_score": 0,
                    "calibrated_score": 0,
                    "score_cap": 0,
                    "media_type": "",
                    "score_adjustment": 0,
                },
            }
            for node_id in ("media", "quality", "dimensions", "anchor")
        },
    }
    dimensions = {"visual_hierarchy": {"grade": 5, "evidence": ["层次清晰"]}}
    scoring = {
        "score": 70,
        "level": "L3",
        "media_key": "other",
        "media_penalty": -10,
        "aesthetic_dimensions": dimensions,
        "inspiration_aesthetic_score": 92,
        "steps": [
            {
                "step": "b_aesthetic_foundation",
                "score_after": 92,
                "note": "以锚点评测美感基础分 92",
            },
            {
                "step": "media",
                "score_after": 82,
                "note": "媒介类型 other，固定扣分 -10",
            },
        ],
    }
    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(
        review_revision=0,
        correction_history_json="[]",
        workflow_node_outputs_json=json.dumps(fixture_outputs, ensure_ascii=False),
        precheck_json="{}",
        aesthetic_json="{}",
        scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
        score=70,
        level="L3",
        reviews=[],
    )
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=19,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json="{}",
    )
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=25,
        run_id=19,
        asset_id=103,
        evaluation_id=100,
        evaluation=evaluation,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "predicted_level": "L3",
                "authoritative_score": 70,
                "scoring": scoring,
                "workflow_node_outputs": fixture_outputs,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )

    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)
    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in view["nodes"]}

    assert by_key["workflow.media.media_type"]["current_value"] == "other"
    assert by_key["workflow.media.score_adjustment"]["current_value"] == -10
    assert by_key["workflow.quality.score_cap"]["current_value"] is None
    assert by_key["workflow.dimensions.dimensions"]["current_value"] == dimensions
    assert by_key["workflow.dimensions.total_score"]["current_value"] == 92
    assert by_key["workflow.anchor.calibrated_score"]["current_value"] == 92


def test_rule_node_correction_history_uses_authoritative_old_value() -> None:
    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
        {
            "workflow": {
                "workflow_definition_id": 83,
                "workflow_version_id": 93,
                "version": "34",
                "canonical_hash": "9" * 64,
                "manifest": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                    "nodes": [
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": "level_matcher",
                            "kind": "rule_module",
                            "display_name": "分数汇总与等级撮合",
                            "config": {
                                "module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
                                "rule_module_version_id": 53,
                            },
                            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                        }
                    ],
                    "edges": [],
                },
            }
        }
    )
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract["contract_hash"]
    item.evaluation.workflow_node_outputs_json = json.dumps(
        {
            "level_matcher": {
                "status": "succeeded",
                "output": {"score": 0, "level": "", "reason": "fixture"},
            }
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )
    item.evaluation.scoring_json = json.dumps(
        {
            "score": 70,
            "level": "L3",
            "steps": [
                {"step": "level", "score_after": 70, "note": "分数 70 → L3"}
            ],
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )
    item.evaluation.score = 70
    item.evaluation.level = "L3"
    item.evaluation.correction_history_json = "[]"

    submit_correction_nodes(
        db,
        run=run,
        item=item,
        contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        nodes=[
            {
                "node_key": "workflow.level_matcher.level",
                "human_value": "L1",
                "reason": "人工真值为 L1",
                "evidence": [{"text": "基准样本已确认"}],
            }
        ],
        review_revision=3,
        idempotency_key="authoritative-old-value-0001",
        actor="运营乙",
    )

    event = json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)[0]
    assert event["old_value"] == "L3"
    assert event["new_value"] == "L1"
    db.close()


def test_repeated_rule_module_instances_keep_distinct_append_only_corrections() -> None:
    db, run, item, _ = _submission_fixture()
    contract = _workflow_v3_contract_for_instance_corrections()
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False)
    run.correction_contract_hash = contract["contract_hash"]
    item.result_snapshot_json = json.dumps(
        {
            "workflow_node_outputs": {
                "redline_first": {
                    "output": {
                        "node_instance_id": "redline_first",
                        "output": {"hit": True},
                    }
                },
                "redline_second": {
                    "output": {
                        "node_instance_id": "redline_second",
                        "output": {"hit": False},
                    }
                },
            }
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )

    submit_correction_nodes(
        db,
        run=run,
        item=item,
        contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        nodes=[
            {
                "node_key": "workflow.redline_first.hit",
                "human_value": False,
                "reason": "首次判断误报",
                "evidence": [{"text": "水印来自平台预览层"}],
            },
            {
                "node_key": "workflow.redline_second.hit",
                "human_value": True,
                "reason": "复核规则应命中",
                "evidence": [{"text": "原图仍有不可移除署名"}],
            },
        ],
        review_revision=3,
        idempotency_key="workflow-v3-instance-correction-0001",
        actor="运营乙",
    )

    events = [
        event
        for event in json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)
        if event.get("schema_version") == "contract-node-correction-v1"
    ]
    assert [event["node_key"] for event in events] == [
        "workflow.redline_first.hit",
        "workflow.redline_second.hit",
    ]
    assert [event["node_instance_id"] for event in events] == [
        "redline_first",
        "redline_second",
    ]
    assert [event["module_field_key"] for event in events] == ["hit", "hit"]
    assert [
        event["frozen_asset_identity"]["component_version"]["version_id"]
        for event in events
    ] == [41, 42]
    db.close()


def test_foreach_correction_view_expands_frozen_wildcard_by_item_key() -> None:
    item_contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "case_image",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "score": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "minimum": 0,
                    "maximum": 100,
                    "title": "图片分",
                },
                "details": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "style": {"type": "string", "title": "风格"},
                    },
                    "required": ["style"],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                },
            },
            "required": ["score", "details"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
        {
            "workflow": {
                "version": "1",
                "manifest": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "category_key": "design_case",
                    "nodes": [
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
                            "kind": "foreach_image",
                            "display_name": "逐图片评测",
                            "config": {
                                "item_executor": {"output_contract": item_contract},
                            },
                        }
                    ],
                    "edges": [],
                },
            }
        }
    )
    runtime = {
        "foreach_images": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {
                "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
                "output": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-foreach-output-v1",
                    "items": [
                        {
                            "item_key": "img.1",
                            "ordinal": 0,
                            "status": "succeeded",
                            "output": {
                                "score": 88,
                                "details": {"style": "现代"},
                            },
                        },
                        {
                            "item_key": "img-2",
                            "ordinal": 1,
                            "status": "failed",
                            "error": {"code": "provider_failed"},
                        },
                    ],
                },
            },
        }
    }
    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(
        review_revision=0,
        correction_history_json="[]",
        workflow_node_outputs_json=json.dumps(runtime, ensure_ascii=False),
        reviews=[],
    )
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=27,
        category_key="design_case",
        correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json="{}",
    )
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=28,
        run_id=27,
        evaluation_id=29,
        evaluation=evaluation,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"workflow_node_outputs": runtime}, ensure_ascii=False
        ),
    )

    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)
    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in view["nodes"]}

    assert not any("items[*]" in key for key in by_key)
    score_key = 'workflow.foreach_images.items["img.1"].result.score'
    style_key = (
        'workflow.foreach_images.items["img.1"].result.details.style'
    )
    failed_key = 'workflow.foreach_images.items["img-2"].result.score'
    assert by_key[score_key]["model_value"] == 88
    assert by_key[style_key]["model_value"] == "现代"
    assert by_key[score_key]["metadata"]["field_identity"]["item_key"] == (
        "img.1"
    )
    assert by_key[failed_key]["read_only"] is True
    assert by_key[failed_key]["read_only_reason"] == "foreach_item_failed"
    from app.correction_view import table_correction_view
    assert table_correction_view(build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item, _table=True)) == table_correction_view(view)
